### [1] [A Transport-Information Framework for Continuous-Fiber-Aligned Coarse-Graining Representation Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27811)
*Koretaka Yuge*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 提出一个"输运信息"框架，将粗粒化（CG）刻画为纤维丛上的局部映射操作，把CG输运代价表示为连续表示二阶矩结构与输运度量的收缩，并借助UCN框架给出其信息不可区分性的非任意信息论解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CG可视为丢弃未被保留信息而使表示降维的操作，但与之相关的信息结构尚无系统描述方法。

Method: 针对连续纤维对齐的表示集，用纤维结构将每个表示与连续实现关联，CG定义为到表示上的投影；以局部映射操作将连续概率质量输运到代表点，得到由连续表示二阶矩结构与输运度量收缩表达的输运代价；结合既有的不可避免规范非线性（UCN）框架进行解释。

Result: CG相关输运代价被解释为由CG几何决定的期望KL散度所量化的信息不可区分性，从而获得非任意的信息论意义。

Conclusion: 该框架为描述单个CG操作的信息代价、以及在考虑底层连续实现的前提下比较表示集提供了基础。

Abstract: Consider representation sets of interest whose elements are associated with continuous realizations related to them through coarse-graining (CG). CG can be regarded as an operation that reduces a representation by discarding information that is not retained in its output. However, a systematic description of the information structure associated with CG in general remains to be established. Here, we propose a transport-information framework for continuous-fiber-aligned representation sets, in which each representation is associated with a continuous realization through a fiber structure, with CG defining the projection onto the representation. Within this framework, CG is described through a local-map operation that transports continuous probability mass onto representative points. The resulting transport cost is expressed as the contraction of the second-moment structure of the underlying continuous representation with the transport metric. Building on the previously proposed framework of unavoidable canonical nonlinearity (UCN), this construction provides a non-arbitrary information-theoretic interpretation of the CG-associated transport cost in terms of information indistinguishability, quantified by an expected Kullback-Leibler divergence associated with the CG geometry. The present framework provides a basis for describing information costs associated with individual CG operations and for comparing representation sets while accounting for their underlying continuous realizations.

</details>


### [2] [Classical-to-Quantum Transition in Blackbody Radiation Thermodynamics: Planck's Law without Statistical Assumptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27858)
*J. Wang,G. Casati,G. Benenti*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文研究闭合有限能量物质-电磁场耦合系统的经典动力学，发现能量向高频模式级联使有效温度趋于零，与经典黑体热力学矛盾；通过要求与Stefan-Boltzmann和Wien定律一致定义特征时间t_L，此时不同能量与腔尺寸的经典谱塌缩到普朗克分布，并推测量子动力学局域化在t_L处中止级联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典黑体热力学假定系统处于有限温度平衡态，但闭合有限能量的辐射-物质耦合系统的经典动力学是否真能达到该平衡态尚不清楚；作者希望在不引入任何先验统计假设的前提下，从纯动力学角度理解普朗克分布的起源。

Method: 受Fermi辐射-物质相互作用模型启发，构建闭合、有限能量的物质-电磁场耦合系统并数值/解析研究其经典动力学演化；以与Stefan-Boltzmann定律和Wien定律同时自洽为条件，确定特征时间t_L；比较不同总能量和腔体尺寸下在t_L时刻的经典谱，检验其与普朗克分布的塌缩关系。

Result: 能量逐步转移至越来越高频率的场模式，物质与辐射的有效温度在长时极限下趋于零，与有限温度平衡假设尖锐矛盾；存在特征时间t_L使两条黑体定律同时满足；在t_L时刻，不同能量与腔尺寸得到的经典谱塌缩到普朗克分布上。

Conclusion: 经典动力学无法达到黑体热力学所设想的平衡态，而普朗克分布可作为动力学演化的涌现结果出现；作者推测量子动力学局域化在t_L处中止经典能量级联，从而在无需先验统计假设的情况下给出普朗克谱的动力学解释。

Abstract: Inspired by Fermi's model of radiation-matter interaction, we investigate the classical dynamics of a closed, finite-energy system, in which matter is coupled to an electromagnetic field. We find that energy is progressively transferred to increasingly high-frequency field modes, so that the effective temperature of both matter and radiation approaches zero in the long-time limit - in sharp contradiction with the finite-temperature equilibrium assumed by classical blackbody thermodynamics. Requiring consistency with the Stefan-Boltzmann and Wien laws identifies a characteristic time $t_L$, at which both laws are simultaneously satisfied. Strikingly, classical spectra obtained for different energies and cavity sizes collapse onto the universal Planck distribution at $t_L$. We conjecture that quantum dynamical localization arrests the classical energy cascade at $t_L$, and that the Planck distribution emerges as a dynamical consequence of this arrest - without invoking any a priori statistical assumption.

</details>


### [3] [Thermodynamic Stability of Classical $\mathbb Z_2$ Lattice Gauge Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27938)
*Shunsuke Sengoku,Weiguang Cao,Haruki Watanabe*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文证明在维数 $d$ 的 $\mathbb Z_2$ 格点规范模型（自旋位于 $m$ 维胞腔，$1\le m\le d-2$）中，足够低的有限温度下自由能密度对温度与任意弱局域扰动联合解析，从而给出无需对称性保护的稳定热相，支持低/高温之间存在热相变的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统有限温度相变依赖自发对称破缺，但对称破缺场可抹除相区别（如 Ising 模型），因此需要寻找无需对称性保护的稳定非平凡热相；本文旨在检验格点规范模型中拓扑性质能否在有限温度下提供此类稳定性。

Method: 对固定空间维 $d$ 中自旋置于 $m$ 维胞腔的经典 $\mathbb Z_2$ 格点规范模型（$1\le m\le d-2$），采用低温解析性论证证明自由能密度在温度与扰动强度上的联合解析性；扰动不要求保持内部或规范对称性，可含非对易量子相互作用；另用高温展开给出解析区域。

Result: 在足够低的正温度与足够弱的局域扰动下，热力学自由能密度关于温度和扰动强度联合解析；高温展开给出在任意固定有限扰动强度下延伸至无穷温度的解析区域。

Conclusion: 结果确立了热力学稳定性，并支持"低温和高温区域在无对称性保护情况下仍由热相变分隔"的猜想。

Abstract: Finite-temperature phases are traditionally studied through spontaneous symmetry breaking, but symmetry-breaking fields can remove their distinction, as in the Ising model. This motivates the search for stable nontrivial thermal phases without symmetry protection. For classical $\mathbb Z_2$ lattice gauge models with spins on $m$-dimensional cells in a fixed spatial dimension $d$, we prove joint analyticity of the thermodynamic free-energy density in temperature and perturbation strength at sufficiently low positive temperatures under sufficiently weak local perturbations, whenever $1\le m\le d-2$. Perturbations need not preserve internal or gauge symmetries and may include noncommuting quantum interactions. A separate high-temperature expansion yields an analytic region reaching infinite temperature at any fixed finite perturbation strength. These results establish thermodynamic stability and support the conjecture that low- and high-temperature regimes remain separated by a thermal transition without symmetry protection.

</details>


### [4] [Initial-State-Dictated Dynamical Universality Classes in Quantum Criticality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28365)
*Yu-Rong Shu,Shuai Yin*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出“初始态决定的动力学普适类”概念，以二聚化Heisenberg模型的虚时弛豫动力学为例，证明同一平衡普适类下不同初始态可产生不同的动力学普适类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统相变物理认为普适类由体系对称性与维度等决定，与微观细节及初始条件无关；但非平衡量子临界动力学中初始态是否影响临界标度行为尚缺乏系统分类，作者由此提出初始态是否可定义新的动力学普适类这一问题。

Method: 研究二聚化Heisenberg模型的虚时弛豫动力学，对比“经典”顺磁（随机自旋）初始态与“量子”顺磁（二聚化与反二聚化）初始态下的临界初始滑移行为，并在体相与边界临界动力学两个层面提取临界初始滑移指数。

Result: 不同初始态给出显著不同的体相临界初始滑移指数，即使处于同一平衡普适类；该初始态依赖性亦延伸至边界临界动力学，同一边界普适类下不同初始态对应不同的表面临界初始滑移指数；对反二聚化初始态还发现违背体相与表面临界初始滑移指数常规关系的反常标度律。

Conclusion: 研究确立了由初始态控制的量子临界性动力学分类，突破了传统平衡普适类范式，表明动力学普适类需同时以平衡普适类和初始态类型来标定。

Abstract: The concept of universality class lies at the core of phase transition physics, with each class uniquely characterized by a dedicated set of critical exponents. Here, we introduce the concept of initial-state-dictated dynamical universality classes in nonequilibrium quantum critical dynamics. By studying the imaginary-time relaxation dynamics of the dimerized Heisenberg model, we demonstrate that distinct kinds of initial states can give rise to different dynamical universality classes within the same equilibrium universality class. Specifically, we find that "classical'' paramagnetic (random-spin) and "quantum'' paramagnetic (dimerized and antidimerized) initial states yield drastically different critical initial slip exponents in the bulk. This initial-state dependence also extends to boundary critical dynamics, where different initial states yield different surface critical initial slip exponents even within the same boundary universality class. Remarkably, for the antidimerized initial state, we uncover an anomalous scaling law violating the conventional relation between the bulk and surface critical initial slip exponents. Our results establish an initial-state-controlled dynamical classification of quantum criticality that extends beyond the conventional equilibrium universality paradigm.

</details>

### [5] [Field Theory of the Gutzwiller Projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27050)
*Abhijat Sarma,Cenke Xu,Chong Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文通过路径积分给出Gutzwiller投影的场论描述：投影态普适物理由一个支撑在零宽度时间薄板（ε→0）上、与体态物质场耦合的规范场描述，并据此解析判定各类投影态的普适行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gutzwiller投影是自旋液体与量子霍尔波函数（如Laughlin态）的著名拟设，且便于数值模拟，但其与parton规范理论之间的精确关系长期未厘清。

Method: 构造Gutzwiller投影的路径积分场论：将投影效应表示为宽度ε→0的时间薄板上的规范场与体态物质场耦合；推广至离散对称性以分析投影的SPT态；并进一步推广以描述G-intertwining缺陷（含自对偶缺陷）。

Result: 证明Gutzwiller投影并非U(1) Dirac自旋液体、spinon费米面自旋液体和复合费米液体长程性质的良好拟设；量子自旋霍尔态的Gutzwiller投影仅有准长程序。而当规范场有能隙时（Laughlin态、Z2自旋液体、Luttinger液体），薄板规范理论与完整parton规范理论的普适物理一致。

Conclusion: 投影态普适物理由零宽度时间薄板规范场刻画；其正确性取决于parton规范场是否有能隙，为判断Gutzwiller拟设的适用性提供了场论判据，并可推广至对称性缺陷的描述。

Abstract: The Gutzwiller projection produces a well-known ansatz for spin-liquid and quantum Hall ground-state wavefunctions that is amenable to numerical simulation, with the Laughlin wavefunction being the most celebrated example. A long-standing issue is the precise relation between the Gutzwiller projection and the closely related parton gauge theory. Here, we clarify this issue by providing a field-theoretic description of the Gutzwiller projection. In a path integral description, the universal physics of the projected state is described in terms of a gauge field supported on a temporal slab of vanishing width $ε\rightarrow 0$ coupled to the bulk matter fields. We analytically derive the universal physics of various Gutzwiller projected states. Our construction is also applicable to discrete symmetries, and we use it to analyze various projected SPT states. Among our results, we show that the Gutzwiller projection is not a good ansatz for the long-distance properties of $U(1)$ Dirac spin liquids, spinon Fermi surface spin liquids or composite Fermi liquids, and that the Gutzwiller projection of a quantum spin Hall state has only quasi-long range order. In contrast, in examples where the gauge field is gapped, including Laughlin states, $\mathbb{Z}_2$ spin liquids and Luttinger liquids, we show that the slab gauge theory from Gutzwiller projection reproduces the correct universal physics of the full parton gauge theory. We also present a generalization of our field theory that describes $G$-intertwining defects, with application to self-duality defects of recent theoretical interest.

</details>


### [6] [Spectral signatures of hidden order and hidden quantum critical points](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27060)
*Reja H. Wilke,Henning Schlömer,Sebastian Paeckel,Annabelle Bohrdt,Fabian Grusdt*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文证明掺杂自旋链中的隐藏序及其隐藏量子临界点、无能隙SPT相可通过RIXS/中子散射可测的动力学两点响应函数直接探测，并揭示其谱学指纹源于自旋与电荷自由度的耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐藏序相虽自发破缺对称性，但对局域两点关联函数"不可见"，通常只能靠非局域弦序参量刻画；此前仅在量子气体显微镜中通过非局域观测量探测，而真实固体中多点观测量基本不可及，隐藏序的可观测特征因而缺乏研究。

Method: 选取掺杂自旋链的极简模型，数值计算动力学两点响应函数（对应RIXS与中子散射谱），并结合解析分析追踪谱特征的微观来源及自旋—电荷自由度的相互作用机制。

Result: 数值上识别出隐藏序相、隐藏量子临界点以及一类无能隙SPT相的清晰谱学指纹；解析上阐明了这些指纹的微观起源，即源自自旋与电荷自由度的耦合。

Conclusion: 谱学（RIXS、中子散射）可作为隐藏序与无能隙SPT相的实用探针，并为这些非常规量子相的低能物理提供统一理解。

Abstract: Hidden-order phases elude the conventional Ginzburg-Landau framework: although they spontaneously break symmetry, their order remains invisible to standard local two-point correlators. Instead, they are typically characterized by nonlocal (string) order parameters and are believed to underlie a range of exotic quantum states, such as symmetry-protected topological (SPT) phases. While hidden correlations have previously been detected through nonlocal observables in quantum gas microscope experiments, in realistic solid-state systems multipoint observables are typically inaccessible and the signatures of hidden order remain largely unexplored. In this work, we show that hidden order in doped spin chains can be directly probed through dynamical two-point response functions measurable by resonant inelastic X-ray scattering (RIXS) and neutron scattering. Focusing on a minimal model, we numerically identify clear spectral fingerprints of both the hidden-order phase, its associated hidden quantum critical point as well as a specific group of gapless SPT phases. Complementing our numerical results with analytical insights, we uncover the microscopic origin of these spectral signatures and demonstrate how they emerge from the interplay between spin and charge degrees of freedom. Together, our results establish spectroscopy as a practical probe of hidden order and gapless SPT phases and provide a unified understanding of the low-energy physics governing these unconventional quantum phases.

</details>


### [7] [Chiral magnetic excitations in the RMn6Sn6 kagome metals (R=Tb, Ho, Er, and Y)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27430)
*Christopher J. Ranlett,Tianxiong Han,S. X. M. Riberolles,Dhurba R. Jaishi,Tyler J. Slade,Bing Li,A. Sapkota,D. L. Abernathy,Rebecca L. Dally,J. W. Lynn,B. G. Ueland,R. J. McQueeney*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文通过非弹性中子散射(INS)证明手性磁激发在RMn6Sn6(R=Tb,Ho,Er,Y)中普遍存在，与磁结构类型无关，且仅在布里渊区L=odd处出现，层内为同一手性、层间反相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TbMn6Sn6的INS研究发现了伴随kagome层的反常手性磁激发，具有巡游特征、大能标(>100 meV)与强阻尼，但局域矩线性自旋波理论无法预测其来源，机理不明。作者希望厘清其起源及与磁结构、Mn局域矩取向的关系。

Method: 对R=Tb、Ho、Er、Y的RMn6Sn6系列化合物系统开展INS测量，这四种材料分别对应单轴、倾斜、面内铁磁与面内双螺旋磁结构；通过比较不同磁结构下的谱学特征，考察手性模式强度与偏振特性。

Result: 手性激发在所有磁结构中均存在，说明其不依赖长程磁序构型；Er与Y(面内磁结构)中手性模式强度更弱，表明激发横向于局域Mn矩方向；模式仅出现在布里渊区L=odd位置，层内手性手性一致、层间相差π反相。

Conclusion: 手性磁激发是RMn6Sn6体系的普适内禀特征，其强度对各向异性磁结构敏感但存在性不受影响，指向kagome层内局域的手性激发机制而非磁序驱动的起源，为理解该体系巡游-局域交织的磁激发提供约束。

Abstract: Recent inelastic neutron scattering (INS) investigations of the kagome metal TbMn6Sn6 reveal novel chiral magnetic excitations associated with the kagome layer. These excitations appear to have an itinerant character with a large energy scale (> 100 meV) and heavy damping; however, the origin of these excitations, which are not predicted by local moment linear spin-wave theory, is not understood. Here, we use INS to show that chiral excitations are also found in RMn6Sn6 compounds with R=Tb, Ho, Er, and Y having uniaxial, tilted, planar ferrimagnet, and planar double-spiral magnetic structures, respectively. Thus, the chiral excitations exist regardless of the magnetic structure. Additionally, R=Er and Y demonstrate that the chiral mode intensity is weaker in planar magnetic structures, suggesting that excitations are transverse to the local Mn moment direction. The chiral excitations appear only at L=odd positions in the Brilloun zone, and correspond to chiral spin exciations of the same handedness within each kagome layer, and pi out-of-phase between layers.

</details>


### [8] [Finite-temperature spectral functions of lattice polarons with process tensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27582)
*Qi Gao,Yuan Wan*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 提出基于过程张量（process tensor）的方法，用于计算晶格极化子的有限温度谱函数，将声子影响泛函表示为低秩矩阵乘积态的逐元素指数，从而高效求和电子世界线，并用嵌入构造一次性获得整个模拟时间区间的传播子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限温度下晶格极化子（如Holstein模型）的谱函数计算在数值上具有挑战性，现有方法（如有限温度DMRG）在多维情形下受限，需要更高效的数值框架。

Method: 1）过程张量方法：将有限温度声子影响泛函写成低秩矩阵乘积态（MPS）的逐元素指数形式，实现电子世界线的高效求和；2）嵌入（embedding）构造：把不同传播时间的世界线嵌入到单个MPS中，从而在单次计算中得到完整模拟时间区间上的传播子；3）以一维Holstein极化子与已有有限温度DMRG结果对标，并进一步计算二维Holstein极化子的谱。

Result: 在一维Holstein极化子上与已有有限温度DMRG结果吻合良好；成功获得二维Holstein极化子数值收敛的谱。谱显示随温度升高，谱权重从相干谱特征逐步向非相干背景转移。

Conclusion: 该过程张量方法为晶格极化子有限温度谱提供了一种高效、可数值收敛的计算途径，并可推广到二维体系；结果揭示了升温导致谱权重由相干向非相干成分转移的物理图像。

Abstract: We develop a process tensor approach for calculating the finite-temperature spectral functions of lattice polarons. We show that the finite-temperature phonon influence functional can be expressed as the elementwise exponential of a low-rank matrix product state, enabling an efficient summation of electron worldlines. An embedding construction embeds worldlines of different propagation times into a single matrix product state. This construction yields the propagator over the full simulated time interval in a single calculation. We benchmark this approach against available finite-temperature density-matrix renormalization group results for the one-dimensional Holstein polaron. We further obtain numerically converged spectra for the two-dimensional Holstein polaron. The spectra reveal a progressive transfer of spectral weight from coherent spectral features into an incoherent background with increasing temperature.

</details>


### [9] [Orbital Magnetization within Self-Consistent Hartree-Fock via an Envelope Theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27624)
*Daniel E Parker*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文用纯实空间方法，结合1960年代发展的磁微扰论与变分法中的包络定理，简洁推导了平均场（自洽Hartree-Fock）层次下的轨道磁化公式，并讨论其与投影位置算符、量子几何的联系及数值实现。


<details>

Abstract: The interplay between topology and quantum criticality has established symmetry enriched criticality as a new paradigm beyond conventional frameworks in condensed matter and statistical physics. However, the conditions under which such criticality emerges remain poorly understood. In this work, we show that a broad class of symmetry enriched critical points separating symmetry protected topological (SPT) and spontaneous symmetry breaking (SSB) phases is governed by Kennedy Tasaki (KT) self duality. At these criticalities, the KT transformation that interchanges the SPT and SSB phases acts as a self-dual noninvertible symmetry, endowing the critical points with nontrivial topological characteristics. We numerically demonstrate this mechanism in $\mathbb{Z}_{n} \times \mathbb{Z}_{n}$ cluster qudit chains and further argue that it naturally extends to higher dimensions, providing a duality based criterion for identifying symmetry-enriched criticality across dimensions.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Single-particle finite-size errors remain a central obstacle in quantum Monte Carlo (QMC) simulations of periodic systems, conventionally reduced via twist averaging (TA). Here we introduce Good-$Γ$ (GG) particle numbers as system sizes $N$ at which the $Γ$-point Hartree--Fock (HF) energy of the high-density two-dimensional uniform electron gas (2D UEG) lies anomalously close to its thermodynamic-limit (TL) value. All results in this work concern the 2D UEG in its high-density Fermi-liquid regime, $r_s=1$--$4$. We systematically investigate the kinetic- and exchange-energy errors, $ΔKE(N)$ and $ΔEX(N)$, that underlie this one-body finite-size-error cancellation across four densities and both spin polarizations. We then compare diffusion Monte Carlo (DMC) energies extrapolated to the TL from GG-$N$ sequences against twist-averaged extrapolations at the same densities and polarizations, finding agreement to within a fraction of a millihartree in every case, independent of whether a $1/N$ or an $N^{-5/4}$ extrapolation form is used.

</details>

Abstract: Away from half filling and above its Neel temperature, the three-dimensional Hubbard model is a paramagnetic metal with antiferromagnetic spin fluctuations, where controlled momentum-resolved results have been lacking. At U/t = 4 and toward half filling the spin fluctuations become strong: for n = 0.7-0.9 at T/t = 0.14 they enhance the susceptibility peak up to sevenfold and raise the momentum-dependent part of the scattering rate to four to five times its second-order value. They are driven by an effective spin interaction screened to about 2U/3 and nearly uniform over the zone. Yet the peak height is set by the slightly smaller value of this interaction at the peak wave vector, and near magnetic order this small difference changes the height by a large factor. Two-particle self-consistent theory reproduces the peak because its sum rule fixes its single constant interaction near this value, but for n = 0.7-0.9 at T/t = 0.14 its self-energy overestimates the local scattering rate and underestimates the momentum-dependent part. Though strong, the fluctuations are short ranged, with a correlation length of at most about two lattice spacings, and for n = 0.7-0.9 and T/t >= 0.14 the scattering rate near the Fermi surface varies by at most about 1% at fixed band energy, without hot spots. At U/t = 4 dynamical mean-field theory (DMFT) reproduces the local scattering rate and quasiparticle weight within a few percent, and the nonlocal part it misses is approximately a function of the band energy. Strong spin fluctuations thus coexist with nearly isotropic quasiparticles, and the results provide a benchmark for extensions of DMFT and for spin-fluctuation theories.

</details>

Abstract: SrRuO$_3$ is a canonical itinerant ferromagnet that continues to serve as a fertile platform for exploring spin-orbit coupled transport and topological responses in correlated oxide metals. We uncover two principal findings. First, the resistance above the Curie temperature $T_{c,on}\approx 165$ K exhibits strong anisotropy, consistent with directionally dependent spin-fluctuation scattering. Below $T_{c,on}$, this anisotropy nearly vanishes, despite persistent structural inequivalence, due to isotropic electron-magnon and electron-electron scattering processes. Second, the application of a magnetic field reveals a striking directional contrast in the linear magnetoresistance (MR): it remains negative and saturates along [001], but changes sign and becomes positive along $[1\bar{1}0]$ below $T_0=145$ K. Abstract: Anyon condensation transitions remain challenging to characterize especially when the condensing anyons are non-abelian. The construction applies when the anyons participating in the condensate have integer quantum dimensions. The second gives a criterion for identifying which point and loop excitations become critical at a continuous transition. The key step is to determine which anyon loops remain condensed throughout. </details>

Result: LG* 型跃迁并非仅由 $\mathcal{A}$ 中任意子的场内容决定，其立方相互作用被 $\mathcal{A}$ 的乘法结构完全固定；对于任意子内容相同但乘法结构不同的孪生代数，得到的是彼此不同的增殖跃迁。

The SymTFT bulk is constructed from the Müger center of the subcategory generated by the algebra, i.e. the category of transparent lines, which can be bosons or fermions. Properties of the transition can then be inferred directly from the algebra classification data. Abstract: We investigate a nearest-neighbor Ising model on the kagome lattice coupled to elastic lattice distortions. Abstract: We introduce a real-frequency framework for the quantum embedding of solids, based on sum-over-poles (SOP) representations. We partition the lattice into periodically repeated interacting fragments and derive an embedded Dyson equation for the fragment, to be solved self-consistently. The resulting scheme generalizes the algorithmic-inversion method of our previous work to interacting Hamiltonians and is closely related to dynamical mean-field theory (DMFT), especially when an extended impurity problem is introduced. By exploiting the compact pole expansion of the embedding potential, we carry out the self-consistent procedure close to the real axis, which avoids ill-posed analytic continuation. In addition, the SOP representation allows us to introduce a pole-and-residue dynamics, which we use to reach the self-consistent solution. After deriving the method, we benchmark it against exact-diagonalization implementations of DMFT on the one-dimensional Hubbard ring at half filling. The framework offers a natural route to real-space dynamical quantum embedding, for periodic systems as well as for strongly correlated defects in materials.

Abstract: Continuous-time Quantum Monte Carlo (CTQMC) impurity solvers are a numerically exact, but expensive, option for solving the Dynamical Mean Field Theory (DMFT) equations, with statistical noise from undersampling presenting a major challenge to low temperature studies. This noise is especially egregious within the sampled self-energy, which can destabilize the self-consistency loop upon insertion into the Dyson equation. In this work, we introduce a neural denoiser for fermionic imaginary time Green's functions based on the diffusion model architecture trained on synthetic data. The network predicts positive and normalized auxiliary spectral functions consistent with the noisy imaginary axis CTQMC data, which is then refined via autodifferentiation to match the measured self-energy, its first two high-frequency moments, and impurity occupation similar to the Constrained Residual Minimization (CRM) method. This leads to Green's functions and self-energies that are causal by design. Through this denoising framework we are able to reduce the required CTQMC measurement steps by a factor of 10-100. We benchmark our method with a half-filled Hubbard model on a Bethe lattice, a two-patch Dynamical Cluster Approximation (DCA) of the two-dimensional Hubbard model, and the SrVO$_3$ system. We reproduce the Mott transition upon increasing Coulomb interaction for the half-filled Hubbard model with only 5000 CTQMC measurements, the pseudogap transition for the DCA two-dimensional Hubbard model with only 5$\times$10$^5$ measurements, and match the spectral function of SrVO$_3$ with 50 times fewer measurements than the reference. Since we generate auxiliary spectral functions, we recover the approximate spectral functions of the systems for free without performing analytic continuation.

Abstract: Rényi multi-entropy extends the Rényi entropy to pure states of more than two parties. It is defined by a replica contraction at integer replica number $n$, and the multi-entropy is its $n\to1$ limit. In holography this limit is conjectured to compute the area of a minimal soap-film web in the bulk. Understanding multi-entropy for general states, however, is harder. Unlike the bipartite case, the replica contraction is not a moment of a density matrix, and the continuation to $n=1$ has to be found by other means. We study this problem for qubit graph states. We show that at every odd $n$ the contraction equals $2^{-k/2}$, where $k$ is a sum of ranks of matrices over a finite field, one row and one column per qubit. We define a class of graphs that we call collision-controlled, for which the rank sum is a polynomial in $n$. This class includes graphs without even cycles and all complete bipartite graphs. The natural polynomial $n \to 1$ continuation is a finite sum over partitions of the parties. Abstract: We demonstrate a hybrid quantum-classical approach for exploring ground-state manifolds of maximum independent set problems, in which quantum hardware supplies the initial solutions and an entirely classical post-processing algorithm expands them, increasing the solution diversity of quantum hardware measurements by up to several orders of magnitude. Quantum optimization devices, while capable of generating high-quality solutions, can suffer from fundamental sampling limitations. This is particularly problematic for applications that strongly depend on a large variety of uncorrelated optimal solutions. We apply the Houdayer cluster update algorithm to systematically expand quantum-generated solution sets through classical post-processing. We validate our approach on a test bed of 43 problem instances from QuEra's Aquila analog annealing device, achieving diversity amplification factors, defined as the increase in the number of distinct solutions at a given set size, of up to 11923x with a median of 24.3x across different problem scales and hardness parameters. Because exact ground-state degeneracies are known for these instances, the recovered fraction of the manifold can be measured rather than bounded: post-processing recovers a median of 21% of the ground-state manifold, and provably all of it for six instances. We also demonstrate that, in some cases, the classical post-processing can discover independent sets larger than those found by the quantum hardware, thus improving on solution quality and not just diversity. While the method is non-ergodic and cannot guarantee complete manifold coverage, it consistently discovers a larger number of high-quality, and in some cases strictly better, configurations than quantum sampling alone. Result: 建立了跨尺度的统一功能分类体系，表明五种基本功能在各物理平台普遍复现；明确量子能量科学相对量子信息科学的判据差异——完整量子态并非必须保持，工程化耗散与退相位可成为资源而非缺陷；给出存储、路由、转换三类能力的度量口径与目标性能情景，用于界定技术相关的门槛。

Conclusion: "量子能量科学"可作为统摄多个子领域的整合性研究纲领，其核心是主动利用量子态、耦合、集体效应与工程化环境调控能量状态与通路。该框架为跨平台比较、性能基准设定与技术路线判断提供共同语言，并提示应重视耗散工程与跨尺度功能迁移的研究价值。

Abstract: A growing research community spanning quantum batteries, molecular and excitonic energy transport, polaritonics, quantum materials and nuclear control has begun to describe its work in terms of quantum energy or quantum energy science. What connects these otherwise disparate efforts is the deliberate use of quantum states, couplings, collective effects and engineered environments to control energetic states and pathways. A key difference from quantum information science is that the complete quantum state does not always need to be preserved; engineered dissipation or dephasing can even improve the desired outcome. We organize this emerging domain through a framework that connects mechanisms, functions, capabilities, and applications. We identify five elementary functions (Excite, Hold, Release, Transfer, and Transform) and examine the mechanisms that implement them. The same functions recur from the molecular to the nuclear scale, although their relative implementation difficulty can differ sharply. Moving toward technology, we highlight three engineering capabilities (Storage, Routing, and Conversion) through the performance metrics that matter for their applications. Stretch scenarios illustrate what performance targets could make these capabilities technologically consequential.

We show that the second-order cumulant expansion fails to capture the finite-size dynamics, while a stochastic version of the Truncated Wigner Approximation (TWA) is remarkably accurate, particularly in the large atom ensemble limit. Using the TWA and the Monte Carlo Wave Function (MCWF) approach, we identify three distinct dynamical regimes of transient DTC behavior in the finite-size Dicke model. Abstract: With the rapid advancement of quantum computing, the security of traditional cryptographic systems is facing significant challenges. As a fundamental component of symmetric cryptography, block ciphers have attracted increasing attention regarding their quantum security in recent years.

First, we construct an 8-round quantum distinguisher for New Structure IV and perform an $r(r>12)$-round quantum key-recovery attack. Compared with Li et al.'s qCPA-based result presented at INSCRYPT 2024, our attack reduces the time complexity by a factor of $2^{n/2}$, where $n$ denotes the subkey length. The proposed quantum distinguisher covers one more round than Liu et al.'s automated-search distinguisher at ASIACRYPT 2025, which results in a $2^{16}$ reduction in the time complexity of our $r$-round quantum key-recovery attack on LBlock. Furthermore, by analyzing the structure of the round function and the key scheduling of LBlock, we reduce the subkey guess space for the 15-round attack from 76 bits to 52 bits, lowering the time complexity from \(O(2^{38})\) to \(O(2^{26})\) by a factor of \(2^{12}\).

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 基于多项-二项损失模型的贝叶斯联合推断是无偏且通用的光子丢失缓解方案，兼容碰撞输出、随数据量扩展、可给出不确定性量化，优于回收法与后选择法。

Abstract: Photon loss is the dominant noise source in linear optical quantum circuits. Each lost photon turns an ideal n-photon outcome into a pattern with fewer photons, so the detector counts no longer follow the ideal output distribution, and the lossless events that do follow it become exponentially rare as the photon number grows. Discarding the lossy events (postselection) therefore wastes most of the data at realistic loss rates, and the ideal distribution has to be inferred from the lossy counts themselves. Recycling-based mitigation (Mills & Mezher, Quantum 2026; Quandela's photon_recycling) reweights lossy samples to recover lossless probabilities, but its estimators are biased and restricted to the no-collision sector, where each output mode holds at most one photon. We instead treat the raw counts as Multinomial observations under per-photon binomial loss and infer the ideal output distribution and the loss rate jointly from a Bayesian posterior, which extends naturally to outputs with collisions. On data from the independent Perceval simulator at the scale of prior work (n=4 photons in m=20 and m=10 modes), the posterior has lower KL divergence than both recycling and postselection at every tested loss rate in the no-collision benchmark. In the collision regime it recovers the loss rate and the collision-pattern probabilities, which recycling cannot estimate, and stays below recycling at every loss rate and below postselection except at low loss, where the two are comparable. Its advantage over recycling grows with the sample count, and it supplies credible intervals that the baselines lack.

</details>

Abstract: Stabilizer Rényi moments depend on a choice of operator basis and need not be determined by entanglement spectra. In this paper, we study their spatial decay in critical quantum chains described by two-dimensional conformal field theories. We derive an exact entanglement baseline for reductions of pure stabilizer states and a symmetry selection rule for the charged contribution at fixed support. An exact Ising example shows that an on-site rotation changes the full decay exponent while preserving every subsystem entanglement spectrum. For growing Ising intervals at index two, we obtain an exact Pfaffian representation and compare the charged amplitude with a candidate conformal boundary calculation. The data support this prediction, but its identification with the lattice limit still requires endpoint matching and control of the order of limits.

</details>

Abstract: Coexistence of classical and quantum light in optical fiber is a key requirement for scalable quantum networks. However, it is fundamentally limited by nonlinear crosstalk from intense classical signals. Hollow-core fiber (HCF), with its intrinsically low optical nonlinearity, offers a promising solution. Here, we experimentally demonstrate the coexistence of polarization-entangled photons with dense wavelength-division multiplexed (DWDM) classical signals with a total launch power of 11.2 dBm, and perform a direct comparison between equal-length single-mode fiber (SMF) and photonic-bandgap-based HCF deployed in a campus network. We observe that HCF suppresses background noise by approximately two orders of magnitude compared to SMF, while preserving high-fidelity entanglement distribution. Our results provide clear experimental evidence of the advantage of HCF and establish its potential as a transmission medium.

Abstract: Universal information extracted from entanglement at a critical point is commonly associated with the long-distance properties of the bulk state, while microscopic details of a lattice bipartition are expected to be washed out under coarse graining. Counterintuitively, we show directly that such microscopic information can survive in the statistical ensemble that determines the second Rényi entropy and obviously change the physics there. Using quantum Monte Carlo simulations of the square-lattice $J$-$Q_3$ model at the Néel--valence-bond-solid (VBS) transition, we measure magnetic and dimer order parameters in the Rényi-2 ensemble. For a standard axis-aligned bipartition, the VBS component perpendicular to the entanglement boundary remains finite in the thermodynamic extrapolation and is inequivalent to the parallel component, while the Néel order vanishes. For a $45^\circ$ tilted bipartition, in contrast, the two VBS components remain equivalent within numerical accuracy. We interpret this contrast in terms of the Rényi twist defect: the two bipartitions have the same bulk theory but realize different microscopic defect symmetries. Our results demonstrate that lattice information invisible to the bulk continuum theory alone can remain active in the long-distance Rényi ensemble, providing a microscopic explanation for the strong bipartition dependence previously observed in entanglement studies of deconfined criticality.

</details>

Abstract: We design a hybrid quantum-classical computational workflow, combining density functional theory and dynamical mean-field theory, to investigate the electronic properties of CaCuO2, a parent structure of high-temperature cuprate superconductors. Our approach uses a quantum algorithm as an impurity solver that is based on variational quantum compressed time evolution to compute the time-dependent Greens function on an IBM Heron quantum device. Despite the significant challenges rooted in the residual imprecision of currently accessible NISQ devices, we successfully resolve the characteristic features of the spectral function. However, we find that the required numerical accuracy for full DMFT self-consistency exceeds current hardware capabilities, indicating that practical real-material DMFT applications will necessitate post-NISQ hardware developments.

Abstract: We advance the compact dressed Hamiltonian (CDH) framework to simulate the dynamics of open quantum systems strongly coupled to structured bosonic environments. The CDH approach combines reaction-coordinate mapping with a polaron transformation, incorporating strong system-environment hybridization into a dressed representation before truncating the collective bosonic mode. As a result of this truncation, dynamical features are captured with substantially fewer states than in the original representation. The residual environment is treated using a weak-coupling Markovian quantum master equation. We benchmark the method for spin-boson-type models including a single spin, coupled spins, and spin ensembles, considering both resonant and off-resonant regimes and weak to strong system-bath coupling. Comparisons with hierarchical equations of motion and converged reaction-coordinate calculations demonstrate that the CDH representation systematically approaches the reference dynamics with a compact Hilbert space, accurately reproducing oscillation frequencies, relaxation behavior, and long-time populations over a broad parameter regime. The method further captures cooperative phenomena, including bath-mediated interactions, synchronization between spins, and signatures for collective emission. Beyond efficient time propagation, the CDH framework provides direct access to the spectrum and eigenmodes of the Liouvillian. We demonstrate that slow dynamical modes converge rapidly in the CDH representation, enabling identification of relaxation timescales without fitting time-dependent observables. This superiority is due to the effectiveness of the proposed algorithm in finding and weighting paths with non-zero amplitude in an extremely sparse path space. Sum of paths algorithms exhibit very favourable space complexity. Monte Carlo replaces exhaustive enumeration of the exponentially large path space by stochastic sampling, the computational cost being determined by the variance of the estimator. This work demonstrates these two features by presenting results on very large quantum circuits, with up to 4096 qubits.

In the one-photon model, the overlap between coherent Juddian branches vanishes in the thermodynamic or infinite frequency-ratio limits, asymptotically suppressing the FOT cusp. In the two-photon model, squeezed Juddian states lose normalizability at spectral collapse, thereby terminating the physical FOT line at finite $N$. Although the cusp strength tends to zero as the endpoint is approached, the terminating FOT retains a collective spectral signature: the different-parity gap is predicted to close with an $N$-dependent exponent $μ_N=(2N+3)/4$, one greater than the cusp termination exponent. By contrast, the same-parity soft mode retains $N$-independent square-root scaling and governs the critical response. Abstract: We theoretically investigate narrowband nonreciprocal transmission in a Doppler-broadened five-level M-type atomic medium. Abstract: The Unruh effect links acceleration to thermalization, providing a relativistic setting for anomalous quantum relaxation. Abstract: Geometric phase gates with addressed laser radiation are widely used in trapped-ion quantum computers to drive high-fidelity entangling gates on an arbitrary qubit pair of a larger ion chain. Such programmable connectivity exploits the collective phonons to mediate the desired interaction, while simultaneously achieving robustness to their thermal fluctuations by a careful calibration of the gate time with respect to the laser and vibrational-mode frequencies. Crosstalk errors can arise when the spectator qubits, which should ideally remain idle during the gate, are partially driven by the laser fields. The minimal crosstalk, which is set by the laser beam size on the spectator ions, can be achieved when the addressing beams propagate perpendicular to the ion chain. However, this results in exciting the radial phonons, and can complicate achieving high-fidelities as the ion chain grows due to the so-called spectral mode crowding. In this work, we present the theoretical description of a scheme recently implemented by Faorlin et $al.$. This scheme overcomes these complications by engineering addressable state-dependent forces along the ion-chain with laser beams that propagate perpendicular to it through the use of structured light gradients. In this way, one can excite the axial phonons and avoid the spectral mode crowding, while simultaneously minimizing the crosstalk. We present a detailed derivation of the entangling gate scheme, together with analytical closed-form expressions for the leading contribution to the gate error caused by thermal motional fluctuations and dephasing noise. Our results confirm that such fluctuations account for up to 93$\%$ of the error budget under the operating conditions of the gate implemented by Faorlin et $al.$. We identify target parameters to reduce the motional error from the reported $10^{-3}$ to the $10^{-5}$ level.

Conclusion: 该框架为COW-QKD提供统一的实验复现、受控安全模型比较、基于模型的协议优化与模块化对抗性压力测试接口，并将暗端口残差确定为关键模型敏感观测量。

Abstract: Practical security analysis of Coherent-One-Way (COW) quantum key distribution requires optical-device modeling, finite experimental statistics, and security inference to be treated consistently without conflating assumptions. We present a modular observable-level simulation framework organized through the pipeline θ_phys -> p -> O -> S_j(O), in which physical source, channel, receiver, and detector parameters determine detector-resolved event probabilities, a finite-block layer applies an effective dead-time treatment and generates aggregate detector counts, and interchangeable security evaluators act on the same observable record. The framework is verified against analytical optical, virtual-state, and finite-key relations and evaluated against reported experimental COW-QKD operating points. The Kato finite-key calculation reproduces the refined experimentally reported secure-key rates most closely at intermediate distances, with deviations of -1.5% at 50 km and -10.3% at 75 km. Monitoring bright-port decoy counts agree within 0.9-2.4%, while remaining distance-dependent dark-port residual identifies the principal model-sensitive observable of the present physical description. Applying Kato and Azuma-Hoeffding concentration treatments to identical detector records isolates the effect of finite-key statistical inference and shows substantially tighter Kato phase-error bounds for rare-event monitoring records studied here. Holding the Kato evaluator fixed, joint optimization of coherent-state mean photon number mu and receiver splitting fraction t_B increases model-predicted secure-key rate by 7.4-20.2% relative to optimization of mu alone with t_B = 0.3. The framework therefore provides a common computational interface for experimental reproduction, controlled security-model comparison, model-based protocol optimization, and modular adversarial stress testing in COW-QKD.

Motivation: 相干资源理论主要针对单态，而量子通信中的自然对象是态系综，系综相干理论虽在发展，但其操作意义（operational meaning）尚未充分刻画。作者希望为系综相干给出定量的操作解释。

Method: 1）以无基底（basis-free）相对熵系综相干为度量，寻找其紧上界并构造达到该上界的系综；2）分析后选择测量（postselective measurement）相对Holevo量的有效信息优势与系综相干的关系；3）将该界应用于qubit B92 QKD协议，推导渐近密钥率上界。

Result: 证明按归一化Haar测度分布的纯态连续系综在任意有限维均为最大相干系综，达到无基底相对熵系综相干的紧上界；证明对于两个等概率纯态，系综相干限制后选择测量相对Holevo量的有效信息优势；给出B92协议中Alice系综相干对渐近秘密密钥率的上界关系。

Conclusion: 系综相干在量子通信与量子密码中具有定量的操作意义：它既是最大相干系综的刻画量，又约束后选择测量的信息优势，并为B92协议的渐近密钥率提供上界。

Abstract: The resource theory of coherence quantifies the quantum properties of states and their role in quantum information processing. Extending coherence theory to ensembles, the natural objects in quantum communication, remains an active research direction, with their operational potential still being explored. In this work, we establish a maximally coherent ensemble in every finite dimension: the continuous ensemble of pure states distributed according to the normalized Haar measure attains the tight upper bound on basis-free relative entropy ensemble coherence. We then prove that ensemble coherence bounds the effective informational advantage of postselective measurements over the Holevo quantity for two equiprobable pure states. Finally, for the qubit B92 QKD protocol, we show that the coherence of Alice's ensemble upper-bounds the secret key rate in the asymptotic regime. These results give ensemble coherence a quantitative operational meaning in quantum communication and cryptography

</details>


### [79] [Quantum Broker: QoS-Aware Distribution of Quantum Computations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28342)
*Giuseppe Bisicchia,Alessandro Bocci,Jose Garcia-Alonso,Juan Manuel Murillo,Antonio Brogi*

Main category: quant-ph

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云量子计算平台在量子比特技术、校准状态、排队行为和定价模型上高度异构，电路可在多个后端执行，但成本、时延、保真度和可复现性取决于后端选择与测量散粒分配方式，缺乏工程化的执行管理手段。

Method: 模块化执行架构 + 可替换的后端画像（profiling）策略 + 声明式 QoS 规范语言 QQSpec；调度规划由两个求解器后端支撑：混合整数线性规划（MILP）与非线性双退火（dual-annealing）求解器。

Result: 在基准量子电路与含噪模拟后端上的实验表明，框架能在异构后端条件下生成可行的 QoS 感知调度方案，相较单后端与朴素多后端基线提升了 QoS 满足度；同时揭示规划时间与调度质量之间的权衡，非线性求解器分配质量更高但计算开销更大。

Conclusion: Quantum Broker 为碎片化量子云基础设施上的可移植、可复现、QoS 感知量子软件提供了可扩展的执行管理层。

Abstract: Cloud-accessible quantum computing has made heterogeneous Quantum Processing Units (QPUs) available through provider platforms that differ in qubit technology, calibration state, queueing behavior, and pricing model. This heterogeneity makes execution management a quantum-engineering concern: a quantum circuit may be executable on several backends, but the resulting cost, latency, fidelity, and reproducibility depend on backend selection and on how measurement shots are allocated. This paper presents the Quantum Broker, an open-source and provider-agnostic framework for Quality-of-Service-aware quantum execution management. The framework operationalizes shot-wise distribution by exploiting the statistical independence of quantum measurement shots to dispatch a single circuit across multiple heterogeneous QPUs according to user-defined constraints and optimization objectives. It combines a modular execution architecture, interchangeable backend-profiling strategies, and the Quantum QoS Specification Language (QQSpec), a declarative language for expressing QoS requirements. Dispatch planning is supported by two solver backends: a mixed-integer linear-programming (MILP) solver, and a nonlinear dual-annealing solver. Experiments on benchmark quantum circuits and noisy simulator backends show that the Quantum Broker can produce feasible QoS-aware dispatch plans under heterogeneous backend conditions and can improve QoS satisfaction with respect to single-backend and naive multi-backend baselines. The results also expose a practical trade-off between planning time and dispatch quality, with the nonlinear solver producing higher-quality allocations at increased computational cost. Overall, the Quantum Broker provides an extensible execution-management layer for engineering portable, reproducible, and QoS-aware quantum software over fragmented quantum cloud infrastructures.

</details>


### [80] [CO$Φ$ - COmpiling PHotonics into dIgital quantum circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28349)

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光子（连续变量）与量子比特（数字）量子计算长期被视为两种独立范式，缺乏统一描述与互译手段，阻碍了在量子比特计算机上模拟量子光学线路（尤其是低光子数区域的Gaussian Boson Sampling）。

Method: 引入单模光场Fock空间截断，构建相位旋转、位移、压缩和分束等基本光子操作的映射表示；给出N=2与N=4截断维度下的显式量子线路构造；对实现的操作进行基准测试并界定适用范围与局限；开发Python工具COΦ，将Blackbird程序编译为OpenQASM线路。

Conclusion: 该映射方法可在量子比特量子计算机上模拟量子光学线路，特别适用于低光子数Gaussian Boson Sampling；但受Fock空间截断限制，仅在有限维度内有效。

Abstract: Photonic and qubit-based (digital) approaches to quantum computing are largely regarded as separate paradigms. This article aims to bridge these paradigms by developing a method for mapping quantum-optical systems onto qubit-based quantum circuits.
  The key ingredient enabling this mapping is the introduction of a truncation of the Fock space describing single-mode optical states. Within this framework, representations of basic photonic operations are developed, including the phase-rotation operator, the displacement operator, the squeezing operator, and the beam splitter. Explicit quantum circuit constructions are provided for Fock-space truncations of dimension $N=2$ and $N=4$. We discuss benchmarking of the implemented operations and identify the regime of applicability and the limitations of the proposed method.
  The presented results can be used to simulate quantum optical circuits on qubit-based quantum computers, with special relevance to Gaussian Boson Sampling in the low-photon-number regime. To demonstrate the practical applicability of this approach, we complement the theoretical framework with a Python-based quantum programming tool CO$Φ$ that compiles Blackbird quantum-optics programs into qubit-based OpenQASM circuits using a truncated Fock-space encoding. The compiler is available on GitHub at https://github.com/Quantum-Cosmos-Lab/cophi.

</details>


### [81] [Hidden parabolic subgroups of $\mathrm{GL}_n$ over finite chain rings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28368)
*Muhammad Imran*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文给出了在任意有效表示的有限链环 R 上，寻找 GL_n(R) 隐藏抛物子群的有界误差量子多项式时间算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐藏子群问题是量子算法的核心问题之一，但非阿贝尔群（如 GL_n 在链环上）的隐藏子群问题尚缺乏有效算法。本文旨在扩展可解范围，覆盖 GL_n(Z/NZ) 及数环上的推广。

Method: 引入"探针"（探测用的辅助群元素或子群）概念；当链长≥2时，用初等阿贝尔 socle 探针先恢复模极大理想意义下的完整旗；当剩余域大小 q≥3 时递归重建旗，逐位提升秩一末端成员，中间数位归约为阿贝尔隐藏子群问题，末位用子集学习与傅里叶采样解决有限域多重平移问题；q=2 时改用通过同余滤子提升共轭抛物子群，最终用线性系统确定其模正规化子方向。

Result: 算法以至少 1-ε 的概率输出生成元，时间复杂度为 poly(n, log|R|, log(1/ε))。推论包括对每个 N≥2 求解 GL_n(Z/NZ) 的隐藏抛物子群，以及在显式极大序表示下求解 GL_n(O_K/N O_K)（K 为数域）。

Conclusion: 该工作将非阿贝尔隐藏子群问题从有限域推广到有限链环上的 GL_n，并借助链环的分层结构与降维归约，为更广代数群上的量子可解性提供了框架。

Abstract: We give a bounded-error quantum polynomial-time algorithm for finding hidden parabolic subgroups of $\mathrm{GL}_n(R)$ over any effectively presented finite chain ring $R$. We call auxiliary group elements or subgroups used to extract information through oracle queries \emph{probes}. For chain length at least two, elementary abelian socle probes first recover the complete flag modulo the maximal ideal. When the residue field has size $q\ge3$, we reconstruct the flag recursively, lifting rank-one terminal members one digit at a time. The intermediate digits reduce to abelian hidden subgroup problems, while the final digit is a finite-field multiple-shift problem solved using learning from subset and Fourier sampling. For $q=2$, where distinct flags can have the same stabilizer, we instead lift the conjugate parabolic subgroup through the congruence filtration. A final linear system determines it modulo normalizer directions. The algorithm outputs generators with probability at least $1-ε$ in time $\mathrm{poly}(n,\log|R|,\log(1/ε))$. Consequences include $\mathrm{GL}_n(\mathbb{Z}/N\mathbb{Z})$ for every $N\ge2$ and, with an explicit maximal-order presentation, $\mathrm{GL}_n(\mathcal{O_K}/N\mathcal{O_K})$ for number fields $K$.

</details>


### [82] [Stable charged energy and phase transitions in an anisotropic Dicke quantum battery with Kerr nonlinearity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28373)
*Shao-Fan He,Lv-Ting Gong,Fu-Quan Dou*

TL;DR: 研究含Kerr非线性的各向异性Dicke量子电池，揭示多重近简并使储能分别由遍历-非遍历转变（ENET）与一、二阶耗散相变（DPT）主导，并用李乌维尔谱理论与Wigner函数刻画临界减缓，借助强化学习优化充电。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子多体系统的相变为调控量子电池（QB）性能提供途径，但各向异性Dicke型QB中Kerr非线性如何重塑能级结构、并经由相变影响充电过程尚不清楚。

Method: 分析Kerr非线性对腔内能级间距结构的重塑；采用李乌维尔谱理论量化DPT相关的临界减缓；用Wigner函数表征临界附近光场性质；以强化学习（RL）优化充电过程，并从遍历/非遍历相动力学角度解释增强机制。

Result: 在特定非线性强度下出现多重近简并；远离该区域时稳定储能主要由ENET支配，区域内则由一阶和二阶耗散相变控制；RL优化带来性能提升，其机制可由遍历与非遍历相动力学解释。

Conclusion: 基于本征相变行为可实现对量子电池性能的调控与优化，为QB优化提供可行路径。

Abstract: Phase transitions (PTs) in quantum many-body systems offer a powerful route to manipulate the performance of quantum batteries (QBs). Here, we investigate an anisotropic Dicke QB with Kerr nonlinearity, focusing on the role of PTs in the charging process of the QB system. By analyzing how the Kerr nonlinearity reshapes the cavity level-spacing structure, we find that multiple near-degeneracies emerge at specific nonlinearity strengths. Away from the region of multiple near-degeneracies, the stable stored energy is governed primarily by the ergodic-nonergodic transition (ENET), whereas within this region it is controlled by first- and second-order dissipative phase transitions (DPTs). We employ Liouvillian spectral theory to quantify the critical slowing down associated with the DPTs, and use the Wigner function to characterize the properties of the cavity field in the vicinity of criticality. We optimize the charging process via reinforcement learning (RL) and reveal the physical mechanism underlying performance enhancement from the perspective of ergodic and nonergodic phase dynamics. Our results offer a feasible pathway for manipulating and optimizing quantum battery performance based on intrinsic PT behaviors.

</details>


### [83] [Scrambling Separates Superradiance from Quantum Chaos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28412)
*Long-Jie Li,Li-Li Gao,Zi-Cheng Liu,Zi-Min Li,Murray T. Batchelor*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 区分全局混沌与局域不稳定性，化解Dicke模型超辐射与量子混沌关系的矛盾结论：FOTOC探测初态所选不动点的局域稳定性，可定量重构谱奇点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期Dicke模型研究将超辐射与量子混沌关联，而后续各向异性Dicke与Rabi研究得出看似冲突的结论，需要厘清超辐射与混沌之间究竟是何种关系。

Method: 在等方Dicke路径上固定低激发能扫描，跨越超辐射边界及邻近混沌窗口；对比各向异性Dicke模型中的相区与混沌性质；引入保真度乱序关联子（FOTOC）刻画初态波包所选不动点的局域稳定性，并结合不动点拓扑与局域态密度关系分析谱奇点。

Result: 等方Dicke路径上超辐射边界与混沌窗口相邻，解释了已报道的关联；各向异性Dicke模型可在正常相混沌、在超辐射相正则，故超辐射既非全局混沌的必要也非充分条件。FOTOC显示超辐射转变使原点失稳，改变各向异性可恢复其稳定性并产生位移鞍点；即使处于经典正则、Poisson统计的Dicke能量壳上，以鞍点为中心的波包仍呈FOTOC指数增长。该局域敏感性结合谱奇点振幅可在无需拟合态密度的情况下重构。

Conclusion: 超辐射与量子混沌的关联源于能量壳与不动点的几何关系而非普适因果；FOTOC作为局域不稳定性探针，既可解释冲突结论，又能实现无需拟合的谱奇点定量谱学。

Abstract: Early studies on the Dicke model linked superradiance to quantum chaos, whereas later anisotropic Dicke and Rabi studies reached apparently conflicting conclusions. We resolve this tension by distinguishing global chaos from local instability. Along the isotropic Dicke path, a sweep at fixed low excitation energy crosses both the superradiant boundary and a nearby chaotic window, explaining the reported correlation. However, the anisotropic Dicke model can be chaotic in the normal phase and regular in the superradiant phase, making superradiance neither necessary nor sufficient for global chaos. A fidelity out-of-time-order correlator (FOTOC), in contrast, probes the local stability of the stationary point selected by the initial wave packet. The superradiant transition destabilizes the origin, whereas a change in anisotropy can restore its stability and create displaced saddles. Wave packets centered at these saddles exhibit exponential FOTOC growth even on a classically regular Dicke energy shell with Poisson statistics. This local sensitivity also enables quantitative spectroscopy: combined with the topology of stationary points and local density of states relations, FOTOC dynamics reconstructs the absolute amplitudes of spectral singularities in both models without fitting the density of states.

</details>


### [84] [Chaos and scrambling in anisotropic Dicke and Rabi models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28415)
*Long-Jie Li,Li-Li Gao,Zi-Cheng Liu,Zi-Min Li,Murray T. Batchelor*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 通过研究同一模型的两种极限（热力学极限下的各向异性Dicke模型与大频率比极限下的各向异性Rabi模型），作者发现混沌与量子scrambling的对应关系在两者间呈现"对应—破坏"的对比，并将差异归因于经典自由度数目不同、相似性归因于本征态离域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子物理中"经典混沌的量子印记"是核心问题，而量子scrambling为此提供了新视角。然而混沌与scrambling之间的对应关系是否普遍成立、在何种条件下失效，尚不清楚。作者希望借助同一模型的不同极限来检验这一对应的稳健性。

Method: 选取各向异性Dicke模型（ARM）作为统一框架，分别考察其热力学极限（各向异性Dicke模型）与大频率比极限（各向异性Rabi模型），比较两种极限下的混沌判据（经典自由度/能谱统计）与scrambling行为（本征态离域），并分析二者对应关系的变化。

Result: 热力学极限下混沌与scrambling高度对应；而在大频率比极限下该对应关系破裂。两种极限混沌行为的差异源于经典自由度数目不同，而相似的scrambling行为则源于本征态离域。因此在大频率比极限下，仅凭本征态离域不足以将ARM判定为经典混沌模型。

Conclusion: 量子系统中识别混沌需同时考虑经典自由度与本征态离域两个要素；单靠本征态离域（scrambling）不足以作为经典混沌的充分判据，这为混沌—scrambling对应关系划定了适用边界。

Abstract: Identifying the quantum signatures of classical chaos remains a central question in quantum physics, and quantum scrambling provides a new perspective on this correspondence. We study two distinct limits of the same model to examine how the correspondence between chaos and scrambling changes across different regimes. The thermodynamic-limit anisotropic Dicke model exhibits a close correspondence between chaos and scrambling, whereas this correspondence breaks down in the large-frequency-ratio limit of the anisotropic Rabi model (ARM). The contrasting chaotic behavior in the two limits originates from the different numbers of classical degrees of freedom, whereas their similar scrambling behavior stems from eigenstate delocalization. This implies that, in the large-frequency-ratio limit, eigenstate delocalization alone is insufficient to qualify the ARM as a classically chaotic model. This finding suggests that identifying chaos in quantum systems requires jointly considering the classical degrees of freedom and eigenstate delocalization.

</details>


### [85] [Quantum Simulation of the Static Yukawa Model via Effective Particles on a Trapped-Ion Quantum Simulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28453)
*Qingfeng Wang,Christopher G. Yale,Edward C. Tortorici,Brian K. McFarland,Daniel Lobser,Melissa C. Revelle,Andrew J. Landahl,Susan M. Clark,Peter J. Love*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在QSCOUT离子阱量子计算平台上实现静态Yukawa模型的量子模拟，并利用相似性重整化将实验可及的低玻色占据数据映射到高占据的有效粒子区域。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子场论中的玻色-费米子相互作用需重整化才能得到有限结果；同时，实验装置中玻色子占据数读出范围有限，难以直接研究强耦合、高占据区域。

Result: 成功在离子阱qubit-boson器件上演示了静态Yukawa模型的时间演化，并借助相似性重整化实现了从有限读出范围数据向强耦合、高占据区间的外推研究。

Conclusion: 相似性重整化可作为实验受限条件下研究高占据、强耦合量子场论模型的有效手段，展示了近期量子硬件在耦合qubit-boson模拟上的可行性。

Abstract: Trapped-ion, neutral-atom, and circuit QED quantum computers naturally enable coupled qubit-boson simulations. Boson-fermion interactions arise in quantum field theory, where renormalization is required to obtain finite results. We study one of the simplest examples of a coupled fermion-boson system, the static Yukawa model, on the QSCOUT trapped-ion system. We use similarity renormalization to express the same physics in terms of effective particles described by different values of the renormalization parameter $s$. This enables us to map data taken in the window of bosonic occupancy that is experimentally accessible to larger occupancies of distinct effective particles. We use this to study strongly coupled, high-occupancy regimes despite limited readout ranges. Using a trapped-ion qubit-boson device on Sandia National Laboratories' QSCOUT platform, we demonstrate the time evolution of the static Yukawa model.

</details>


### [86] [Tuning between collective dynamics and scrambling through initial-state symmetry alignment in long-range spin models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28462)
*Sridevi Kuriyattil,Johannes Kombe,Andrew J. Daley,Pablo M. Poggi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究确定性平移不变的远程 XY 自旋模型，发现通过初始态的局域扰动可使动力学在集体（可积平均场）与快速扰乱之间切换，且耦合图邻接矩阵谱可高效预测快速扰乱的起始。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远程相互作用体系中，既有可用对称全连接可积平均场描述的集体动力学，也有无序远程作用导致的量子信息快速扰乱，二者通常出现在不同模型。作者希望厘清这两类动力学能否在单一确定性、平移不变的模型中同时出现，并给出控制二者切换的判据。

Method: 构建长程耦合的 XY 自旋模型，包括稀疏的 2 的幂次（PWR2）图（每个自旋对数级邻居）与幂律衰减耦合两种情形；以完全对称态为基准，通过局域扰动改变初态；用二分与多分纠缠度量刻画动力学，用半经典极限下的 Lyapunov 指数刻画交叉，并用耦合图邻接矩阵的谱性质预测快速扰乱的起始。

Result: 仅改变初态（局域扰动对称态）即可使动力学由集体行为转变为扰乱行为，该转变在二分与多分纠缠度量上可被观测；半经典 Lyapunov 指数刻画了集体—扰乱交叉；邻接矩阵谱性质是预测快速扰乱出现的高效工具。

Conclusion: 该工作为在单一确定性长程 XY 模型中统一描述并调控集体与扰乱动力学提供框架，且该设置可在腔中中性原子或光镊阵列实验中实现。

Abstract: Long-range interactions in many-body quantum systems give rise to a wide range of dynamical phenomena, from collective dynamics well described by integrable mean-field models with symmetric all-to-all couplings, to fast scrambling of quantum information from disordered long-range interactions. Here, we investigate scenarios in which both regimes emerge within a single deterministic, translation-invariant XY spin model with long-range couplings. We consider a sparse powers-of-two (PWR2) graph, where each spin interacts with a logarithmic number of neighbors, and a power-law-decaying model. By altering the initial state via local perturbations on a completely symmetric state, we show that the dynamics changes from collective to scrambling as witnessed by bipartite and multipartite entanglement measures. We characterize this collective-to-scrambling crossover using Lyapunov exponents in the semiclassical regime, and show that the spectral properties of the coupling graph adjacency matrix provide a very efficient tool to predict the onset of fast scrambling. Our results offer a unified framework for controlling the switch between collective and scrambling dynamics in a setup accessible in experiments with neutral atoms in cavities or in tweezer arrays.

</details>


### [87] [NeST: ultra-high-rate qLDPC codes with sub-$50μ$s stabiliser cycles via spin shuttling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28538)
*Adam Siegel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出NeST（嵌套穿梭轨道）架构，首次将量子LDPC码与非阿贝尔群几何和硅自旋量子比特硬件协同设计，在方形晶格上用刚性传送带式运动实现码的校验置换，获得高速率、亚50微秒稳定子周期与超过10^-3的电路级阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子LDPC码在有用距离下编码率可达50%，但其校验是长程群作用而非局域邻域，此前该连通性依赖中性原子（毫秒级、自由空间光镊置换）。硅自旋量子比特通过电子穿梭提供同类原语（量子比特块的相干输运），且为微秒级，但运动受限于 fabricated 轨道、须为无碰撞的物理晶格运动。因此需要码与硬件的协同设计。

Method: 构建几何上模仿一族非阿贝尔群的架构：在普通方形晶格的穿梭轨道上布置层级嵌套的传送带环路，使每个群乘法对应整个量子比特列车的全局受控刚性运动。在该族上进行码搜索，并进行包含穿梭退相干的电路级模拟；另为[[150,30,10]]码构造码手术gadget，其中每个量子比特仍仅作刚性传送带运动。

Result: 码搜索得到速率20%的mitten码，最高达[[1400,280,≤28]]；电路级模拟给出阈值高于10^-3，稳定子周期低于50μs，比中性原子实现快2–3个数量级。外推估计最大码在穿梭演示提升10倍、其他物理错误p=0.1%时，每比特每轮逻辑错误率可达10^-14。码手术gadget零量子比特开销，门层数增加29%。

Conclusion: 超低开销容错与快速时钟并非相互竞争的目标；硅自旋量子比特可同时提供二者，NeST架构证明了码—硬件协同设计在量子LDPC码实用化路径上的可行性。

Abstract: Quantum low-density parity-check codes now reach encoding rates up to 50% at useful distances. Their checks however are long-range group actions, not local neighbourhoods, and that connectivity has so far been supplied by neutral atoms. Silicon spin qubits offer the same primitive --- coherent transport of qubit blocks --- through electron shuttling, and on microsecond rather than millisecond timescales. They however travel along fabricated tracks rather than free space: permutations an atom array obtains by moving tweezers must here become collision-free motions on a physical lattice. We here present the first code and spin-qubit hardware co-design. We build an architecture whose geometry mimics that of a family of non-abelian groups, broad enough to host codes of excellent parameters: a hierarchy of nested conveyor-belt loops on a plain square lattice of shuttling tracks, in which every group multiplication becomes a rigid, globally controlled motion of whole qubit trains. We call it NeST, for Nested Shuttling Tracks. A code search over the family returns rate-20% mitten codes up to $[[1400,280,\leq 28]]$, and circuit-level simulations including shuttling dephasing give thresholds above $10^{-3}$ with sub-$50μ$s stabiliser cycles, two to three orders of magnitude faster than neutral-atom implementations. Via extrapolations, we estimate that this largest code may reach a logical error probability per qubit per round of $10^{-14}$, assuming a ten-fold improvement on early shuttling demonstrations, with other physical errors at p=0.1%. Logic is compatible with NeST too: for the [[150,30,10]] code we build a code-surgery gadget in which every qubit again moves only by rigid belt motions, at no qubit cost and a 29% increase in gate layers. This demonstrates that ultra-low-overhead fault tolerance and a fast clock speed are not competing goals, and that silicon spin qubits can supply both.

</details>


### [88] [Complete Modified Logarithmic Sobolev Inequalities for one-dimensional quantum Spin Chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28600)
*Li Gao,Jingyu Guo,Sihao Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 证明了一维自旋链上Chen–Kastoryano–Gilyén Gibbs采样器在任意固定正温度下满足常数与系统尺寸无关的完全修正对数Sobolev不等式，给出O(log N + log(1/ε))的迹与钻石混合时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gibbs采样器的混合时间分析中，混合时间常数常随系统尺寸N增长；本文旨在建立常数与N无关的完全修正对数Sobolev不等式（CMLSI），以得到尺寸无关的快速混合保证，并覆盖非交换与非均匀相互作用情形。

Method: 针对有界有限程相互作用的哈密顿量，证明完全空间熵的因子分解（complete spatial entropy factorization），并将准局域（quasi-local）误差吸收为原始联合态与参考态下的条件相对熵，从而导出CMLSI。

Result: 在任意固定正温度下得到常数与N无关的CMLSI，适用于含非交换、空间非均匀相互作用的一维自旋链；推出迹混合时间与钻石混合时间均为O(log N + log(1/ε))；静态估计同时给出单点Petz热浴及正则化热浴动力学的尺寸无关CMLSI。

Conclusion: 该工作为局部Gibbs采样器提供了尺寸无关的快速混合理论保证，方法上的熵因子分解与条件相对熵吸收技巧可推广至相关量子采样与热化分析。

Abstract: We prove a complete modified logarithmic Sobolev inequality with a system-size-independent constant for Chen--Kastoryano--Gilyén Gibbs samplers on one-dimensional spin chain at every fixed positive temperature. The result holds for Hamiltonians with bounded finite-range interactions, including noncommuting and spatially nonuniform interactions. It yields trace and diamond mixing times of order $O(\log N+\log(1/ε))$. The proof establishes complete spatial entropy factorization and absorbs quasi-local errors as conditional relative entropy at the original joint state and reference. The static estimate also yields a system-size-independent CMLSI for the single-site Petz heat-bath and regularised heat-bath dynamics.

</details>
