### [89] [Achieving ultrahigh-temperature superfluidity in atomic Fermi gases by suppressing pairing fluctuations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26997)
*Lin Sun,Qijin Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 提出利用粒子数失配与动量空间限制的协同效应抑制配对涨落，在中等强度吸引下实现三维光晶格中的超高超流转变温度，为室温超导提供新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BCS平均场预测Tc随配对强度增大而升高，但配对涨落在强耦合区会压制Tc，导致无法获得高温超导；因此需要一种能抑制配对涨落的机制。

Method: 在三维光学晶格中引入布居数失配（p≲0.1的中等失配）并结合有限布里渊区带来的动量空间限制；利用配对费米子与多余多数费米子之间的强泡利排斥抬高配对激发能，抑制有限动量配对涨落。

Result: 中等失配下配对色散出现能隙，指数级抑制热凝结物耗竭；获得超高的Tc、增强的凝聚体比例与减小的赝能隙，且所需塞曼场小、避免轨道复杂性。

Conclusion: 该机制适用于任何具有有限布里渊区的晶格，其可观测特征（能隙化色散、凝聚比例增强、赝能隙减小）在现有实验条件下可检验，为室温超导开辟新途径。

Abstract: Achieving room-temperature superconductivity has been a long-sought goal of both the physics community and the industry. The superfluid transition temperature $T_\mathrm{c}$ of a Fermi system initially increases with increasing pairing strength between fermions following the mean-field prediction of the Bardeen-Cooper-Schrieffer (BCS) theory, and is then suppressed by pairing fluctuations as the strength increases further, which prevents $T_\mathrm{c}$ from becoming high. Here we propose a mechanism for suppressing pairing fluctuations using the synergy between population imbalance and momentum space confinement in lattice systems, enabling an ultrahigh $T_\mathrm{c}$ at moderately strong attraction. We show that in a three-dimensional optical lattice, strong Pauli exclusion between paired and excess majority fermions pushes up the pair excitation energy and suppresses finite-momentum pairing fluctuations. The mechanism is general to any lattice with a finite Brillouin zone; the optimal imbalance is modest ($p\lesssim 0.1$), keeping the Zeeman field small and avoiding orbital complications. The gapped pair dispersion at moderate imbalance exponentially suppresses thermal condensate depletion, providing a testable signature. The gapped dispersion, enhanced condensate fraction, and pseudogap reduction are all accessible in present-day experiments. This work opens new avenues toward room-temperature superconductivity.

</details>


### [90] [A high-speed, large-aperture 3D printed laser shutter for cold atom experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27709)
*Samantha L. Rubinstein,Charles W. Woffinden,Maarten T. M. Christenhusz,Guillaume Gauthier,Tyler W. Neely*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可3D打印的低成本光学快门，具备4.5 mm大孔径、399±8 μs切换时间和2.7±0.2 ms延时，速度约7.0±0.1 m/s，适用于冷原子等多快门精密光控实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冷原子实验需要快速、可重复动作的光学快门，而现有方案通常成本高、孔径受限、切换慢，且需对光束进行缩放或精密对准。

Method: 采用3D打印设计制造快门，实现4.5 mm直径大孔径与低成本装配，并对四个不同快门进行切换时间、延时与速度的测量验证。

Result: 全照明孔径下切换时间为399±8 μs，延时2.7±0.2 ms，快门速度测得7.0±0.1 m/s，四个快门表现一致、可重复。

Conclusion: 该方案为冷原子及其他需要多快门、精确光控的光学实验提供了易获取、可扩展的解决方案，且无需对光束进行缩放或孔径精密对准。

Abstract: Cold atom experiments require optical shutters with rapid and repeatable actuation. Existing solutions often involve high cost, limited aperture diameter and slow transitioning. We present a 3D-printable shutter design featuring a large aperture of 4.5 mm diameter, low-cost assembly, and robust performance. The device achieves switching times of $399\pm8~μ$s with delays of $2.7\pm0.2$ ms for a fully illuminated aperture, with four different shutters demonstrating repeatable performance. We measure the shutter speed to be $7.0\pm0.1$ m/s. This approach offers an accessible, scalable solution for cold atom and other optical experiments requiring multiple shutters with precise light control and avoids the need to resize or precision-align a beam through an aperture.

</details>


### [91] [Luther-Emery liquid in one-dimensional attractive Fermi gas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27823)
*Hai-Ying Cui,Yi-Cong Yu,Aashish Kafle,Han Pu,Randall G. Hulet,Thierry Giamarchi,Xî-Wen Guan*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文通过Bethe ansatz精确求解吸引型Yang-Gaudin模型并结合玻色化，确定一维吸引费米气的自旋与电荷低能激发谱，预言Luther-Emery液体（LEL）的自旋能隙在动力学结构因子中表现为尖锐阈值，并提出可用阱中Bragg谱学直接观测LEL。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一维相互作用费米气可承载Tomonaga-Luttinger液体、FFLO配对态及Luther-Emery液体等丰富量子液体。其中LEL（一个自由度无能隙、另一自由度有能隙）虽早有理论预言，但至今缺乏确凿的实验观测，本文旨在为其提供明确的谱学判据与可行的实验平台。

Method: 对吸引型Yang-Gaudin模型采用Bethe ansatz精确求解，完整确定自旋与电荷两个通道的低能激发谱；在此基础上用玻色化方法解析推导有隙自旋激发的动力学结构因子（DSF），并考察囚禁势中的Bragg谱学信号特征。

Result: 对完全配对的费米子，谱结构显示有隙自旋激发与无能隙电荷激发共存，即LEL行为。在阱中气体里，自旋Bragg谱峰频率显著高于电荷模式峰，两峰频率分裂随相互作用强度近似线性变化；更关键的是，自旋DSF中能隙表现为尖锐阈值，阈值以下谱权重完全为零。

Conclusion: 自旋DSF的尖锐阈值构成LEL直接且无歧义的谱学指纹，因此准一维阱中吸引费米气可作为长期寻求的LEL直接观测的理想实验平台。

Abstract: One-dimensional (1D) interacting Fermi gases underpin a rich variety of quantum liquids, ranging from the Tomonaga-Luttinger liquid and the Fulde-Ferrell-Larkin-Ovchinnikov paired state to the Luther-Emery liquid (LEL). Notably, the LEL-characterized by gapless excitations in charge or spin degree of freedom and gapped excitations in the other-has long been theoretically predicted but has eluded definitive experimental observation to date. By exactly solving the attractive Yang-Gaudin model via the Bethe ansatz, we fully determine the low-energy excitation spectra for both spin and charge channels. For fully paired fermions, the spectrum features gapped spin excitation coexisting with gapless charge excitation. We further employ bosonization to derive analytical expressions for the dynamical structure factor (DSF) of the gapped spin excitation, which is a long-standing challenge in this study. In the trapped gas, we find that the spin Bragg spectroscopy peak occurs at a much higher frequency than the charge-mode peak, where the splitting frequency between these two peaks scales approximately linearly with the interaction strength. Crucially, the spin gap manifests as a sharp threshold in the spin DSF, below which spectral weight is completely absent, proving a direct and unambiguous spectroscopic fingerprint of the LEL. Our work establishes quasi-1D trapped attractive Fermi gases as a pristine experimental platform for the long-sought direct observation of the LEL.

</details>


### [92] [Computing excited states of dipolar Bose--Einstein condensates by constrained gentlest ascent dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27878)
*Fu Zheng,Wei Liu,Xu Qian*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 提出基于约束最缓上升动力学（CGAD）的数值方法，配合半隐式正弦伪谱离散、正交化修正与NUFFT加速非局域偶极相互作用，系统计算一至三维偶极BEC任意指定Morse指数的激发态（约束鞍点），并发现激发态在基态平均场不稳定的参数区间内仍可存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偶极BEC（含长程偶极-偶极相互作用）物理丰富，其激发态对应约束鞍点，编码集体激发模式并影响动力学稳定性与量子相变；但相比基态，激发态的数值研究（尤其偶极体系）仍十分匮乏，缺乏能稳定捕获指定Morse指数鞍点的系统算法。

Method: 在平均场Gross–Pitaevskii框架下，采用约束最缓上升动力学（CGAD）稳定地定向求解带归一化约束的约束鞍点，从而取得任意指定Morse指数的激发态；数值上采用半隐式正弦伪谱离散并加入正交化修正步骤，非局域偶极-偶极相互作用项通过非均匀快速傅里叶变换（NUFFT）高效精确求值；将格式应用于一维、二维、三维偶极BEC进行系统数值实验。

Result: 成功在一、二、三维偶极BEC中计算并详细考察了激发态；数值模拟表明，即使在凝聚体基态已呈平均场不稳定的参数区域，激发态仍可持续存在。

Conclusion: 该工作为偶极量子气体激发态研究提供了可扩展的计算框架，并为后续理论与实验研究提供启示。

Abstract: Dipolar Bose--Einstein condensates (BECs), i.e., BECs with long-range dipole-dipole interactions (DDI), exhibit extraordinarily rich physics and serve as a versatile experimental platform for exploring exotic quantum states of matter. Within the mean-field framework, a dipolar BEC can be described by the Gross--Pitaevskii (GP) model with a DDI term. Its stationary states consist of the ground state, which minimizes the GP energy functional under the normalization constraint, and excited states, which are higher-energy critical points that typically correspond to constrained saddle points. Excited states encode collective excitation modes and play a key role in the dynamical stability and quantum phase transitions of cold-atom systems. However, in contrast to the extensively studied ground state, they remain far less explored numerically, especially in dipolar systems. In this work, we develop a numerical method based on the constrained gentlest ascent dynamics (CGAD) that systematically computes excited states of dipolar BECs by stably targeting constrained saddle points with any prescribed Morse index. Specifically, a semi-implicit sine-pseudospectral discretization with an orthonormalization correction step is adopted, and the nonlocal DDI terms are evaluated efficiently and accurately via the nonuniform fast Fourier transform (NUFFT). The resulting scheme is applied to one-, two-, and three-dimensional dipolar BECs, enabling a detailed numerical investigation of their excited states. Remarkably, our simulations reveal that excited states can persist even in regimes where the ground state of the condensate becomes mean-field unstable. This work provides a computational framework for studying excited states in dipolar quantum gases and offers insights for future theoretical and experimental investigations.

</details>


### [93] [Finite-size spin fluctuations and fixed-index sweeps in a ferromagnetic spinor condensate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28163)
*Yi Ding,Junyan Luo,Yixiao Huang*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 在零磁化铁磁自旋-1 BEC中，优化旋转量子Fisher信息在激发态量子相变附近出现凹陷，位置随体系尺度反比偏移，其数值等于2⟨S²⟩，可由半经典轨道平均（按经典态密度归一）描述；谱边缘存在自旋-向列压缩，固定指标激发态扫描的半激发时间约为基态的11–13倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究激发态量子相变（ESQPT）附近量子Fisher信息的行为，并寻找与总自旋涨落、半经典动力学及自旋-向列压缩的关联；同时刻画激发态扫描相对基态的时标差异。

Method: 针对零磁化铁磁自旋-1 BEC，计算优化旋转量子Fisher信息F_max随控制参数ε的演化，考察其在ε_c=-q附近的凹陷及随尺寸N的位移；用2⟨S²⟩对比凹陷值；构建按经典态密度归一的半经典轨道平均公式（含有效截断κ），并与小幂律拟合比较；在保留态中分析自旋-向列压缩；另做固定指标激发态扫描，提取半激发时间与局域尺寸指数。

Result: 凹陷位置随N按反比尺度趋近ε_c=-q；在q=0.6、0.8、1.0、1.2且N至3840时，凹陷值F_max等于2⟨S²⟩；半经典公式在N=120–3840区间最大相对残差低于0.028，小幂律拟合相当；将实测能量偏移直接代入则残差达17–30%，故κ为有效截断；保留态中谱边缘出现自旋-向列压缩；固定指标激发态扫描半激发时间为基态的11.2–13.1倍，且四个尺寸下局域尺寸指数递增。

Conclusion: ESQPT附近的Fisher信息凹陷由总自旋涨落标定，并可由半经典轨道平均（有效截断κ）定量刻画；谱边缘支持自旋-向列压缩，激发态扫描显著慢于基态且尺寸标度呈递增趋势。

Abstract: We find a dip in the optimized rotation quantum Fisher information of a zero-magnetization ferromagnetic spin-1 Bose-Einstein condensate near the excited-state quantum phase transition. Its displacement from $ε_c=-q$ decreases on an inverse-size scale. At $q=0.6,0.8,1.0,1.2$, the dip value of $F_{\max}$ equals $2\langle S^2\rangle$ for $N$ up to $3840$, linking it to total-spin fluctuations. A semiclassical orbit average normalized by the classical density of states describes the dip over $N=120$--$3840$ with maximum relative residual below $0.028$; a small power law fits comparably. Inserting the measured energy offsets directly into the semiclassical formula leaves residuals of $17$--$30\%$, so $κ$ acts as an effective cutoff. Among retained states, spin-nematic squeezing occurs at the spectral edges. Separately, fixed-index excited-state sweeps have half-excitation times between $11.2$ and $13.1$ times their ground-state counterparts, with local size exponents increasing across the four sizes.

</details>


### [94] [Dynamics of two bosons with density-assisted tunnelling in a one-dimensional tilted lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28278)
*Muhammad S. Hasan,Vikash Mittal,Tomasz Sowiński*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 研究倾斜一维光晶格中双玻色子在标准与密度辅助隧穿共同作用下的动力学，揭示两种隧穿相对符号导致的增强/竞争机制及对关联与纠缠的调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密度辅助隧穿与标准隧穿可共存于光晶格体系，二者相对符号如何影响Bloch动力学、关联与纠缠尚不清楚，需在可控少体框架下厘清其相互作用机制。

Method: 在倾斜一维光晶格中构建含标准隧穿与密度辅助隧穿的双玻色子模型，分析两种隧穿相对符号对应的增强与竞争两种区域，考察无相互作用时早期纠缠增长随密度辅助隧穿振幅的标度行为，并引入有限在位相互作用考察对称性破缺与关联增强。

Result: 无在位相互作用时，早期纠缠增长随密度辅助隧穿振幅呈相同标度；有限在位相互作用打破增强与竞争区域间的对称性，并使两区域的关联增强方式不同；在两种隧穿振幅相等时，doublon与singlon扇区对任意在位相互作用、晶格倾斜与初态完全解耦。

Conclusion: 两种隧穿机制的相互竞争与协同可对少体动力学与纠缠进行受控调节，等幅情形下的扇区完全解耦为简化与操控该体系提供了明确条件。

Abstract: We investigate the dynamics of two bosons in a tilted one-dimensional optical lattice with both standard and density-assisted tunnelling. The relative sign of the two tunnelling processes strongly affects the Bloch dynamics, leading to distinct reinforcing and competing regimes. We examine how the tunnelling processes generate correlations and find that, in the absence of onsite interactions, the early-time entanglement growth follows the same scaling with the density-assisted tunnelling amplitude. Finite onsite interaction changes this picture by breaking the symmetry between the reinforcing and competing regimes and enhancing the correlation differently in two regimes. This shows how the interplay between the two tunnelling mechanisms can change dyanmics and entanglement in a controlled few-body setting. Particularly, at equal tunnelling amplitudes, the doublon and singlon sectors decouple completely for arbitrary onsite interaction, lattice tilting, and initial state.

</details>


### [95] [Characterizing supersolid phase transitions in trapped quantum gases with tilted dipoles at finite temperature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28621)
*Julian Kusch,Lauriane Chomaz,Wyatt Kirkby*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文理论研究囚禁偶极量子气体中相变的性质，通过调节接触相互作用强度和偶极取向来绘制相图，并利用虚时滞后分析表征相变的有序性；发现无调制到调制相变的有序性随偶极倾角呈现"不连续—连续—不连续"的演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受近期一项实验工作（arXiv:2608.20327）启发，需要理论阐明偶极量子气体中相变的性质（一阶还是二阶），并解释相变有序性如何受实验可调参数控制，从而为实验研究提供指导。

Method: 采用包含热效应与量子效应的扩展Gross-Pitaevskii方程进行数值模拟，系统扫描接触相互作用强度与偶极取向，绘制包含无调制超流体、三角液滴阵列和条纹态的相图；并通过虚时滞后分析（imaginary-time hysteresis）判定相变的有序性。

Result: 无调制到调制相变随偶极倾角增大由不连续变为连续，在大倾角处又恢复为不连续。连续的程度还受粒子数、温度和囚禁势影响。连续行为通常与条纹态的出现相关联，但该对应关系并非系统性成立，有限尺寸效应也会影响相变的连续性。三角到条纹相变在小接触相互作用强度下为不连续，在靠近无调制—调制相边界时逐渐变得连续。

Conclusion: 有限偶极量子气体中的相变有序性可通过实验参数进行调节，本文给出了相图与相变类型的系统性刻画，并为相关实验观测提供了理论依据与指导。

Abstract: Motivated by a recent experimental work $[\href{https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20327}{\text{arXiv:}2608.20327}]$, we theoretically investigate the nature of phase transitions in a trapped dipolar quantum gas upon tuning the contact interaction strength and dipole orientation. Using extended Gross-Pitaevskii simulations including thermal and quantum effects, we map the phase diagram comprising unmodulated superfluids, triangular droplet arrays, and stripe states. We further characterize the order of the transitions separating them through imaginary-time hysteresis analysis. The unmodulated-to-modulated transition evolves from discontinuous to continuous with increasing dipole tilt before becoming discontinuous again at large tilts. The degree of continuity is further affected by particle number, temperature, and confinement. While continuous behavior generally correlates with the emergence of stripe states, this correspondence is not systematic, and finite-size effects also influence the continuity of the transition. The triangular-to-stripe transition is found to be discontinuous at small contact interaction strengths and becomes increasingly continuous upon approaching the unmodulated-to-modulated phase boundary. Our results reveal a tunable transition order in finite dipolar quantum gases and provide guidance for its experimental investigation.

</details>

### [96] [Zeeman Quantum Geometry of the Layer Pseudo-spin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26982)
*Bingxuan Jiang,Shihao Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 提出了层赝自旋的量子几何（由带间Berry联络与层矩阵元构成的混合张量），揭示其在面内振荡电场下的选择定则与响应。在最小两带模型中，层赝自旋量子度规与Zeeman–Berry曲率非零但整区积分为零，线性响应下空间平均极化不变、仅产生可层分辨成像的莫尔周期胞内图案；对扭转MoTe₂双层定量并识别出高阶净响应的整流、螺旋性奇与三次谐波通道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由位置依赖层间隧穿耦合的二维层具有可控的层赝自旋极化自由度，但其量子几何及其对外场的响应规律尚不明确。层赝自旋为三重旋转下的Ising型标量不变量，其选择定则应与赝矢量自旋不同，需专门刻画。

Method: 引入层赝自旋的量子几何：以带间Berry联络与层矩阵元构造混合张量，研究其对振荡面内电场的响应；在最小两带模型中计算层赝自旋量子度规与Zeeman–Berry曲率及其布里渊区积分；对扭转MoTe₂双层量化局域偶极调制，并通过高阶展开识别整流、螺旋性奇及三次谐波通道。

Result: 最小两带模型中量子度规与Zeeman–Berry曲率非零，但整区积分为零；线性阶面内驱动不改变空间平均极化，仅写入莫尔周期胞内极化图案，可由层分辨隧穿直接成像；扭转MoTe₂双层中量化了局域偶极调制，并发现净响应需通过更高阶的整流、螺旋性奇与三次谐波通道出现。

Conclusion: 层赝自旋具有独特量子几何与选择定则，其响应以局域胞内图案为主、线性净极化为零；通过高阶非线性通道可获得可测净响应，为层分辨探测与莫尔极化调控提供方案。

Abstract: Two-dimensional layers coupled by position-dependent interlayer tunnelling host a layer pseudo-spin whose polarization is a controllable internal degree of freedom. We introduce the quantum geometry of this layer pseudo-spin: a mixed tensor formed from the interband Berry connection and the layer matrix element, which governs the response to an oscillating in-plane electric field. Because the layer pseudo-spin is an Ising-type scalar invariant under threefold rotation, its selection rules differ from those of a pseudovector spin. In a minimal two-band model, the layer-pseudo-spin quantum metric and Zeeman--Berry curvature are nonzero, while their zone integrals vanish. An in-plane drive therefore leaves the spatially averaged polarization unchanged at linear order but imprints a moiré-periodic intracell pattern, directly imageable by layer-resolved tunnelling. For twisted MoTe$_2$ bilayer, we quantify the local dipole modulation and identify the rectified, helicity-odd, and third-harmonic channels through which a net response appears at higher order.

</details>


### [97] [Stacking-Induced Tuning of a Flat-Band Surface State in NbIrTe4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27096)
*Jiabin Yu,Mohamed Oudah,Giang Nguyen,Hsianghsi Kung,V. King,Rysa Greenwood,Adrian F. Rumson,Jisun Kim,Matteo Michiardi,Giorgio Levy,Andrea Damascelli,E. R. Johnson,Meigan Aronson,Lukas Muechler,Sarah A. Burke*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 通过STM、ARPES与DFT研究三元TMDC材料NbIrTe4，发现费米能级下约8 meV的强隧穿共振源自表面派生平带，并对表面局域堆叠序敏感；在顶层横向位移区域该共振变为能隙样特征，并因顶两层态密度空间分布改变而变得色散。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三元层状TMDC具有高可调性并可承载关联与拓扑态，但其电子性质对堆叠序与层间耦合高度敏感。NbIrTe4体态为Weyl半金属候选、单层预测为量子自旋霍尔绝缘体，然而其表面态及堆叠序对电子结构的影响尚不清楚，因此需要实验与理论结合进行系统研究。

Method: 结合扫描隧道显微镜/隧穿谱（STM/STS）、角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）与密度泛函理论（DFT），并辅以准粒子干涉（QPI）测量，对NbIrTe4的表面电子结构与表面态进行表征，对比不同局域堆叠序区域的谱学差异。

Result: 费米能级下约8 meV的强隧穿共振被归因于表面派生的平带，且对表面局域堆叠序敏感；在顶层发生横向位移的区域，隧穿谱呈现能隙样特征，费米能级附近强度发生能量移动。DFT与QPI联合分析表明，该准一维平带表面态附近的谱学剧烈变化源于顶两层态密度空间分布的改变，使原本平坦的能带变得色散。

Conclusion: NbIrTe4可作为一个平台，通过局域结构操控（如顶层横向位移/堆叠序调控）有效调节表面能带色散，为在层状TMDC中实现堆叠与表面对称性驱动的电子态调控提供依据。

Abstract: Ternary layered transition metal dichalcogenides (TMDCs) offer high tunability and access to correlated and topological states. However, like the broader family of layered TMDCs, their properties are sensitive to stacking order and interlayer coupling. Here, we investigate the electronic structure and surface states of NbIrTe$_4$, a candidate Weyl semimetal in bulk, and predicted to be a quantum spin Hall insulator in monolayer form. Using scanning tunnelling microscopy (STM), angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES), and density-functional theory (DFT), we attribute the strong resonance in tunnelling spectroscopy located approximately 8 meV below the Fermi level to a surface-derived flat band which shows sensitivity to the local stacking order at the surface. In regions where the top layer is laterally shifted, tunnelling spectroscopy instead shows a gap-like feature with intensity near the Fermi level shifted in energy. Combining DFT and quasiparticle interference (QPI) measurements, we find this dramatic change in the spectroscopy in the vicinity of the quasi-1D flat-band surface state becomes dispersive due to a change in the spatial distribution of the density of states in the top two layers. These findings establish NbIrTe$_4$ as a platform where surface band dispersion can be effectively tuned through local structural manipulation.

</details>


### [98] [Quantum Oscillations across Hole Crystallization in Rhombohedral Graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27134)
*Daniele Guerci,Enrico Rossi*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 菱面体石墨烯中，内层空穴口袋存在两种竞争归宿：坍缩为单连通费米液体，或结晶为"反晶体"（局域空穴与巡游电子共存）；后者可解释量子振荡中频率突增及多频率谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验在载流子密度降低至超导附近时观测到量子振荡主频突增及额外频率出现；非相互作用能带模型虽能给出多频率，却无法解释频率的上跳现象。

Method: 基于环形费米海（annular Fermi sea）中内层空穴口袋的两种竞争相：口袋坍缩（collapse）为单连通费米液体，与空穴结晶（crystallization）形成反晶体（anticrystal）。对比两者对费米轮廓及磁击穿轨道的不同影响，并与实验振荡谱对照。

Result: 口袋坍缩在固定密度下收缩外费米轮廓；空穴结晶则保留更大的轮廓，从而支持磁击穿轨道。二者对比可解释频率突增，重构轨道产生多音调谱。

Conclusion: 反晶体可作为菱面体石墨烯超导邻近正常态的候选态，为量子振荡异常提供统一解释。

Abstract: Recent quantum-oscillation measurements in rhombohedral graphene reveal an abrupt increase in the dominant frequency and the emergence of additional frequencies as the carrier density is lowered toward superconductivity. Noninteracting band models can account for multiple frequencies but do not explain the upward jump. We show that these features can arise from two competing fates of the inner hole pocket in an annular Fermi sea: collapse into a simply connected Fermi liquid or crystallization into an anticrystal, in which localized holes coexist with itinerant electrons. Pocket collapse contracts the outer Fermi contour at fixed density, whereas hole crystallization preserves a larger contour that supports magnetic-breakdown orbits. This contrast provides a possible explanation for the frequency jump, while additional reconstructed orbits produce a multitone spectrum. Our results support the anticrystal as a candidate normal state near superconductivity in rhombohedral graphene.

</details>


### [99] [Steering of topological criticality in the Su-Schrieffer-Heeger chain via non-Hermitian substitutional impurities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27436)
*Juan A. Cañas,Guo-Qing Zhang,A. Martín-Ruiz*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 研究有限浓度非厄米替代杂质掺杂的一维SSH链，揭示通过破坏杂质跃迁的内部结构对称性可确定性地宏观移动拓扑相边界，并给出有限尺寸标度与谱跃迁特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作多关注无序对拓扑相的影响，但缺乏一种主动、确定性调控拓扑相边界的手段；作者希望利用局域非厄米缺陷的不对称性实现对宏观拓扑保护的定向重构。

Method: 在SSH链中引入位点依赖、非互易的胞内与胞间跃迁振幅作为非厄米替代杂质；通过破坏杂质跃迁的内部结构对称性使临界点发生宏观位移；利用有限尺寸标度分析提取关联长度临界指数，并结合非厄米趋肤效应对谱跃迁进行几何刻画。

Result: 临界点随局部缺陷不对称性可控地扩张或压缩拓扑相；拟合得 ν≈1.455（对称）与 ν≈1.397（非对称），表明偏离纯净SSH普适类，且在高缺陷密度下发散；观察到杂质诱导的实谱到复谱跃迁，并由非厄米趋肤效应刻画。

Conclusion: 孤立的非厄米不对称性可协同作用，确定性地移动宏观拓扑保护边界；局域缺陷不对称性为拓扑相边界的主动调控提供了机制。

Abstract: We investigate the topological phase in a one-dimensional Su-Schrieffer-Heeger (SSH) chain in the presence of a finite concentration of non-Hermitian substitutional impurities. Our primary focus is the discovery of a deterministic mechanism to actively reconfigure the topological phase boundaries by introducing site-dependent, non-reciprocal intracell and intercell hopping amplitudes. Specifically, breaking the internal structural symmetry of the non-reciprocal impurity hoppings induces a targeted macroscopic displacement of the critical point. This mechanism allows for the controlled expansion or compression of the topological phase strictly via local defect asymmetry. We validate this boundary displacement through finite-size scaling analysis. The extracted correlation length critical exponents ($ν\approx 1.455$ and $ν\approx 1.397$ for symmetric and asymmetric configurations, respectively) confirm a disorder-induced departure from the pristine SSH universality class, with the exponent diverging at high defect densities. We also report an impurity-induced real-to-complex spectral transition geometrically characterized by the Non-Hermitian Skin Effect. Ultimately, our results establish how isolated non-Hermitian asymmetries cooperatively interact to deterministically shift macroscopic topological protection.

</details>


### [100] [Ballistic transport and crystallographic control in altermagnetic quantum rings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27173)
*Edilberto O. Silva*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 研究有限宽度 d 波交错磁量子环中相干两端输运：AB 通量保持 h/e 周期并存在精确半通量透射零；垂直磁场按有效面积平移干涉图样，交错磁劈裂将公共零点分成自旋相关节点，晶体旋转交换透射自旋，θ_A=π/4 时完全自旋简并；50 nm 环在亚特斯拉场下可获得 |P_T|>0.99 的窗口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交错磁体兼具铁磁体的自旋分裂能带与反铁磁体的净零磁化，是无需外磁场即可产生自旋极化的候选平台；但其在介观相干干涉器件（量子环）中的输运特征，特别是干涉节点位置、通量周期性与晶向控制的自旋极化之间的关系尚不明确，需要系统研究。

Method: 采用相干两端输运理论，构建有限宽度 d 波交错磁量子环连接非磁性引线的模型；引入包络 AB 通量并叠加垂直磁场，计算透射振幅与透射自旋极化 P_T；进行有限偏压、温度、无序、塞曼项以及自旋守恒电压探针的数值计算，以检验极化稳定性。

Result: AB 通量严格 h/e 周期；对称单模几何下出现精确半通量透射零。垂直场的图样平移对应 A_eff=1.0342πr_0²；交错磁劈裂把单一零点分裂为邻近的自旋相关节点，晶体旋转使透射自旋反转，镜像取向 θ_A=π/4 精确自旋简并。50 nm 参考环在亚特斯拉磁场下出现 |P_T|>0.99 的窗口，其中最易实现者仅需均匀磁场、无需包络通量。有限偏压、热展宽、无序、塞曼与电压探针计算表明极化比单个相干共振振幅更稳定。

Conclusion: 干涉节点位置、磁通周期性与晶体取向可控的自旋极化构成交错磁介观干涉仪的实验可识别特征，为该类器件的自旋干涉调控与探测提供依据。

Abstract: We study coherent two-terminal transport through a finite-width $d$-wave altermagnetic quantum ring with nonmagnetic leads. A confined Aharonov--Bohm flux remains exactly $h/e$ periodic and, in the symmetric one-mode geometry, produces an exact half-flux transmission zero. A perpendicular field shifts the interference pattern according to an effective area $A_{\rm eff}=1.0342\,πr_0^2$ and the altermagnetic splitting resolves the common zero into nearby spin-dependent nodes. Rotating the crystal reverses the transmitted spin, whereas the mirror orientation $θ_A=π/4$ is exactly spin degenerate. For a $50$~nm reference ring, sub-tesla fields yield windows with $|P_T|>0.99$, the most accessible of which requires a uniform field alone and no confined flux. Finite-bias, thermal, disorder, Zeeman, and spin-conserving voltage-probe calculations show that the polarization is appreciably more stable than individual coherent-resonance amplitudes. These results identify interference-node positions, flux periodicity, and crystal-controlled spin polarization as experimentally useful signatures of an altermagnetic mesoscopic interferometer.

</details>


### [101] [Diffusive Description of Superconducting Single-Photon Detectors Using Quantum Mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27189)
*Krzysztof Pomorski,Eryk Halubek*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 利用非厄米量子力学方法，对超导单光子探测器中描述低能与高能准粒子的耦合经典扩散方程动力学进行参数化，维度覆盖一至三维空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导单光子探测器中低能与高能准粒子的输运由耦合的经典扩散方程描述，但此类唯象方程组缺乏统一的、可解析刻画的动力学描述框架，需要引入新的数学工具以参数化和系统化其演化行为。

Method: 将非厄米量子力学的方法论移植到经典扩散体系中，把耦合扩散方程映射为具有非厄米哈密顿量结构的演化问题，从而对其动力学进行参数化处理；分析在一维、二维和三维空间中的相应表述。

Result: 给出了耦合扩散方程在非厄米量子力学框架下的参数化描述，明确该表述可推广至一至三维空间，为低能/高能准粒子子系统之间的耦合动力学提供了统一的形式化处理。

Conclusion: 非厄米量子力学可作为一种有效的形式化工具用于刻画超导单光子探测器中准粒子扩散动力学，所建立的参数化框架具有维度普适性，有望支撑后续对探测器响应与准粒子输运的定量分析。

Abstract: The methodology of non-Hermitian quantum mechanics is employed to parameterize the dynamics of coupled classical diffusion equations describing low- and high-energy quasiparticles in superconducting single-photon detectors in one to three spatial dimensions.

</details>


### [102] [Magnetic-Field-Induced Darkening of Bright Excitons in a MoWSe$_2$ Monolayer Alloy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27469)
*Katarzyna Olkowska-Pucko,Artur O. Slobodeniuk,Mehdi Arfaoui,Elena Blundo,Natalia Zawadzka,Łucja Kipczak,Jan Szpakowski,Diana Vaclavkova,Dipankar Jana,Piotr Kapuściński,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Clement Faugeras,Marek Potemski,Adam Babiński,Antonio Polimeni,Maciej R. Molas*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在Mo₀.₆₈W₀.₃₂Se₂中发现面内磁场导致亮激子"变暗"的互补效应：25 T下亮激子发光被抑制约85%，同时激活低14.2±1.3 meV的暗激子线，用亮-暗混合模型定量描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁场常用于"点亮"自旋禁戒的暗激子，但其逆效应——对光学亮激子的作用——几乎未被研究。作者旨在探索磁场诱导亮激子变暗这一互补现象，并厘清其物理机制。

Method: 选取合金材料Mo₀.₆₈W₀.₃₂Se₂，在4.2 K下施加最高25 T的面内磁场进行磁光光谱测量；采用亮-暗激子混合模型拟合能量与强度的耦合演化，提取有效面内g因子。

Result: 面内磁场达25 T时，在亮激子下方14.2±1.3 meV处激活一条暗激子谱线，亮激子发光被抑制约85%；混合模型定量再现能量与强度的同步演化，得到|g∥|=5.2±0.2。合金化是关键，使体系接近亮-暗交叉点，兼具小亮-暗劈裂与强面内磁耦合。

Conclusion: 该体系处于磁场诱导能级排斥显著抬高亮支、并强烈耗尽其热布居的区间，从而揭示了一条通过调控亮-暗激子层级结构实现激子布居磁调控的通用途径。

Abstract: Magnetic fields are widely used to brighten spin-forbidden dark excitons, but the inverse effect on an optically bright exciton has remained largely unexplored. We demonstrate the complementary phenomenon of magnetic-field-induced darkening of a bright exciton in Mo$_{0.68}$W$_{0.32}$Se$_2$. At 4.2 K, an in-plane magnetic field up to 25 T activates a dark-exciton line 14.2$\pm$1.3 meV below the bright exciton while suppressing the bright-exciton emission by about 85\%. A bright-dark mixing model quantitatively captures the coupled energy and intensity evolution with an effective in-plane $g$ factor of $|g_{\parallel}|=5.2\pm0.2$. Alloying is crucial to the effect, as it brings the system close to the bright-dark crossover, where a small bright-dark splitting is combined with unusually strong in-plane magnetic coupling. This places the system in a regime where field-induced level repulsion raises the bright-like branch and strongly depletes its thermal population. These results identify a general route to magnetic control of exciton populations through engineering of the bright-dark hierarchy.

</details>


### [103] [Magnetic-Field Effects on the Electronic States of the K$_4$ Lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27539)
*Katsuhiro Takahashi,Shouya Yoshida,Katsunori Wakabayashi*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究均匀磁场下K$_4$晶格的电子态：最近邻紧束缚模型结合Peierls替换将问题约化为有效二维问题，得到Hofstadter谱，其自相似亚结构反映晶格在垂直磁场平面上的投影几何；弱场极限下由三狄拉克锥低能有效哈密顿量导出朗道能级结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: K$_4$晶格是非中心对称的三维网络，在Γ和H点具有三狄拉克锥，其磁场响应（Hofstadter谱与朗道能级）此前尚不清楚，作者希望揭示磁场下该晶格的能谱结构及其与投影几何、螺旋轴的关系。

Method: 采用最近邻紧束缚模型加Peierls替换；固定平行于磁场的Bloch动量分量，把三维问题约化为有效二维Hofstadter问题；利用K$_4$网络的螺旋轴使场平行动量充当附加磁通；并在弱场极限下围绕Γ点构建低能有效哈密顿量求解朗道能级。

Result: Hofstadter谱的自相似亚图案反映晶格投影几何：$\bm{B}\parallel[100]$时在通量标度$\phi=1/8$出现类方形子结构，$\bm{B}\parallel[111]$时在更精细标度$\phi=1/32$出现类蜂窝与类三角子结构（均对应每个投影单胞一个磁通量子）；螺旋轴导致的附加磁通可使谱沿通量轴平移，并把H点第二个三狄拉克锥引入磁谱；三狄拉克锥给出三次久期方程，其解显示一个赝自旋平坦支与两个相对论性朗道阶梯共存，并存在沿磁场线性色散的单一手征$n=0$能级。

Conclusion: 三维K$_4$晶格在磁场下的能谱可借投影几何与螺旋轴对称性理解：Hofstadter亚结构的标度由投影晶格决定，场平行动量等效为可调附加磁通；三狄拉克锥的低能物理表现为平坦支、两套相对论朗道阶梯与单一手征零模共存，与晶格数值计算一致。

Abstract: We investigate the electronic states of the K$_4$ lattice in a uniform magnetic field within a nearest-neighbor tight-binding model. The K$_4$ lattice is a \rev{noncentrosymmetric three-dimensional network with triple-Dirac cones at the $Γ$ and H points}. By fixing the Bloch momentum component parallel to the field, the Peierls-substituted tight-binding problem reduces to an effective two-dimensional problem and yields a Hofstadter spectrum \rev{whose self-similar subpatterns reflect the geometry of the lattice projected onto the plane perpendicular to the field}. A square-like substructure emerges on the flux scale $φ= 1/8$ for $\bm{B}\parallel[100]$, and honeycomb- and triangular-like substructures emerge on the finer scale $φ= 1/32$ for $\bm{B}\parallel[111]$\rev{. Here $φ$ is the flux through the elementary plaquette of the projected lattice, and these scales correspond to one flux quantum per projected unit cell. Owing to the screw axes of the chiral K$_4$ network, the field-parallel momentum acts as an additional flux, which, for commensurate values of this momentum, shifts the spectrum along the flux axis and brings the second triple-Dirac cone at the H point into the magnetic spectrum}. In the weak-field limit, we derive the Landau-level structure from a low-energy effective Hamiltonian around the $Γ$ point. The three-component nature of the triple-Dirac cone gives rise to a cubic secular equation, whose solutions describe how a pseudospin-flat branch coexists with two relativistic Landau ladders \rev{and a single chiral $n=0$ level dispersing linearly along the field, in agreement with the lattice calculation}.

</details>


### [104] [Collective Hall quantization in 3D Chern vector systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27802)
*Zhi-Qiang Zhang,Shuguang Cheng,Hongfang Liu,Hailong Li,Hua Jiang,X. C. Xie*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文预言由陈向量 C=(0,m,n) 保护的、具有集体量子化的三维量子霍尔效应，其霍尔电导为 G_xy=±(mL_y+nL_z)e²/h。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维量子霍尔效应以往仅在单一非零陈数分量（单个陈数）情形下确立了量子化输运，而更一般的陈向量态如何保护量子化输运仍是未解的基本问题。

Method: 基于陈向量 C=(0,m,n) 的体拓扑分析，考虑尺寸为 L_y、L_z 的样品，将各边缘模式视为一个统一的手性网络，分析全局拓扑对 mL_y+nL_z 的强制集体量子化，并筛选候选材料。

Result: 发现尽管单个边缘模式对无序脆弱，整体呈现集体量子化；霍尔电导 G_xy=±(mL_y+nL_z)e²/h，同时依赖陈向量分量与样品尺寸，并提出可用于实验观测的候选材料。

Conclusion: 鲁棒量子化是统一手性网络的全息（整体）性质而非单个模式的属性，揭示了一种新的三维输运区制，并给出可实验检验的材料平台。

Abstract: Understanding the three-dimensional (3D) quantum Hall effect, characterized by a Chern vector rather than a single Chern number, has been a central pursuit in topological physics. While quantized Hall transport is established for the special case with one nonzero Chern component, recent realizations of more general Chern-vector states raise a fundamental question: how does such topology protect quantized transport? Here, we predict a distinct 3D quantized Hall effect protected by $\mathbf{C}=(0,m,n)$. For samples with dimensions $L_y$ and $L_z$, global topology enforces collective quantization of $mL_y+nL_z$, despite the fragility of individual edge modes to disorder. This reveals a novel transport regime in which robust quantization emerges as a holistic property of a unified chiral network,rather than of its constituent modes. Crucially, this collective response manifests as a quantized Hall conductance $G_{xy}=\pm(mL_y+nL_z)e^2/h$, with a distinctive dependence on both Chern-vector components and sample dimensions. We propose candidate materials to facilitate experimental observation.

</details>


### [105] [On the origin of nonreciprocal charge transport in topological insulator BiSbTeSe2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27849)
*Fengyi Guo,Ziqi Wang,Xiubing Li,Zheng Dai,Congcong Li,Yufan Ju,Shuai Zhang,Bin Cheng,Zhe Ying,Fengqi Song*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在双栅控BiSbTeSe2拓扑绝缘体器件中观测到高度可栅控的非互易电荷输运：二次谐波纵向电阻随电流和磁场线性变化，在电荷中性点附近最强、两侧符号反转，费米能级进入体态后被强烈抑制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非互易电荷输运可用于探测方向依赖的电荷动力学与自旋-动量锁定电子态，需要寻找可栅控、室温稳定的材料平台。

Method: 制备双栅控BiSbTeSe2拓扑绝缘体器件，测量二次谐波纵向电阻随电流、磁场及栅压的变化，并分析其与费米能级位置（体态/表面态）的关系。

Result: 二次谐波纵向电阻对电流和磁场均呈线性；非互易响应在电荷中性点附近达到最大，两侧符号反转，费米能级进入体态时被强烈抑制；电荷中性点附近的信号比无栅调制时约大一个数量级，且可保持至300 K。

Conclusion: 非互易响应归因于拓扑表面态中自旋-动量锁定的不均匀性；BiSbTeSe2是有前景的室温非线性电子学平台。

Abstract: Nonreciprocal charge transport provides an effective approach for probing direction dependent charge dynamics and spin-momentum-locked electronic states. Here, we report highly gate-tunable nonreciprocal charge transport in dual-gated BiSbTeSe2 topological insulator device. The second-harmonic longitudinal resistance is linear in both current and magnetic field. The nonreciprocal response reaches its maximum near the charge neutrality point, exhibits sign reversals on either side, and is strongly suppressed once the Fermi level enters the bulk states. We suggest that the nonreciprocal response is attributed to the inhomogeneity of spin-momentum locking in the topological surface states. The nonreciprocal signal near the charge neutrality point is about one order of magnitude larger than that without gate modulation and remains robust up to 300 K, highlighting BiSbTeSe2 as a promising platform for room-temperature nonlinear electronics.

</details>


### [106] [Polarity-selective domain wall propagation driven by spin-orbit torque](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28052)
*Nobuyuki Umetsu,Michael Quinsat,Masaki Kado,Hiroki Tokuhira*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在垂直磁化纳米线中，二维各向异性景观的局部几何（如倾斜的各向异性边界）可在给定电流下选择性决定哪种手性畴壁极性能够传播，其机制源于DMI导致的相反极性畴壁获得相反方向的电流诱导倾斜，从而与边界形成不同对准程度和钉扎强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁各向异性的空间变化会阻碍自旋轨道矩驱动的手性畴壁运动，但其如何影响不同极性畴壁的传播选择性尚不清楚，尤其是各向异性景观的局部几何是否以及如何筛选畴壁极性。

Method: 采用微磁学模拟，基于扩散平滑方法生成空间关联的随机各向异性景观；并构造单一倾斜各向异性边界作为隔离机制的最小模型，结合包含DMI的扩展集体坐标模型进行理论分析。

Result: 在同一无序实现中，up–down与down–up畴壁表现出不同的传播结果；在DMI作用下，相反极性的畴壁产生相反方向的电流诱导倾斜，与倾斜边界对准程度不同，导致钉扎强度不同；反转边界倾角可交换被钉扎与可传播的极性；扩展集体坐标模型能复现该几何选择效应，并区分传播阈值与静态钉扎极限。

Conclusion: 二维各向异性景观的局部几何可作为极性选择器，调控自旋轨道矩驱动的手性畴壁传播；该几何选择机制由DMI诱导的极性相关倾斜与边界对准共同决定，并由扩展集体坐标模型定量描述。

Abstract: Spatial variations in magnetic anisotropy impede the spin-orbit-torque-driven motion of chiral domain walls in perpendicularly magnetized nanowires. We show that the local geometry of a two-dimensional anisotropy landscape can select which wall polarity propagates under a given current. Micromagnetic simulations of spatially correlated random anisotropy landscapes generated by diffusion-based smoothing reveal different propagation outcomes for up--down and down--up walls within the same disorder realization. A single inclined anisotropy boundary isolates the mechanism: in the presence of the Dzyaloshinskii--Moriya interaction, opposite wall polarities develop opposite current-induced tilts, producing different alignment with the boundary and hence different pinning strengths. Reversing the boundary inclination interchanges the pinned and propagating polarities. An extended collective-coordinate model captures this geometric selection and distinguishes the propagation threshold from the static pinning limit.

</details>


### [107] [Time-Reversal Symmetry in Artificial Topological Graphene Rings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28055)
*J. A. da Silva Neto,G. Q. Garcia,A. M. de M. Carvalho,C. Furtado*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究带均匀质量项的双谷Dirac哈密顿量描述的人工石墨烯环，在Aharonov-Bohm通量与垂直均匀磁场下的精确谱、持续电流与磁化，并阐明时间反演的物理含义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工石墨烯环兼具Dirac型谷自由度与环形几何，是研究谷简并、轨道磁响应与持续电流的理想平台；但现有工作缺乏对质量项、磁场谷依赖项及时间反演破缺的系统精确处理。

Method: 采用低能双谷Dirac哈密顿量加均匀质量项；AB通量情形化为由Bessel函数求解的二阶径向方程，给出精确久期方程及窄环下的谱与持续电流闭式；加入垂直磁场后用合流超几何函数求解径向问题，去除伪根后由久期行列式的导数给出持续电流与磁化；再做弱场展开（精确至B^4量级）分离抗磁与顺磁贡献，并分析时间反演约束。

Result: 得到AB通量与垂直磁场两种情形的精确久期方程与窄环闭式解；磁场除轨道耦合外还通过Dirac结构产生的谷依赖项解除谷简并；弱场展开显式给出谷劈裂及磁化率的抗磁/顺磁分解；物理时间反演给出相反磁场下能级间的精确关系，而有效单谷时间反演被质量项与边界条件破坏但在零场谱中不留痕迹；数值结果予以验证。

Conclusion: 该工作为人工石墨烯环提供了完整的解析框架，明确区分了轨道效应与谷依赖的Dirac磁效应，并厘清了两种时间反演概念在该体系中的作用与可观测后果。

Abstract: We study an artificial graphene ring described by the low-energy two-valley Dirac Hamiltonian with a uniform mass term. When the ring is threaded by an Aharonov-Bohm flux, the problem reduces to a second-order radial equation solved by Bessel functions. We give the exact secular equation and, for narrow rings, closed expressions for the spectrum and the persistent current. We then add a uniform magnetic field perpendicular to the plane. The radial problem is solved with confluent hypergeometric functions, and the persistent current and magnetization are written as derivatives of a secular determinant from which the spurious roots have been removed. Besides the usual orbital coupling, the field enters through a valley-dependent term that comes from the Dirac structure and lifts the valley degeneracy. A weak-field expansion, accurate up to corrections of order B^4, gives this valley splitting explicitly and separates the diamagnetic and paramagnetic contributions to the susceptibility. Finally, we work out what time reversal implies for the spectrum. Physical time reversal, which exchanges the valleys, leads to an exact relation between levels at opposite fields. The effective single-valley time reversal, on the other hand, is broken by the mass term and by the boundary conditions, yet leaves no trace in the zero-field spectrum. Numerical results for the spectrum, the persistent current, and the magnetization illustrate the analysis.

</details>


### [108] [Adiabatic confinement and number-theoretic spectra of massless Dirac fermions in twisted trilayer graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28063)
*Kunihiro Yananose,Changwon Park,Young-Woo Son*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在扭转三层石墨烯中，Bernal畴内的无质量狄拉克费米子因缺乏进入菱方畴的绝热通道而被完全反射，从而在无质量隙、无边缘的条件下形成三角形量子腔，其态密度振荡编码Eisenstein整数算术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 石墨烯中的无质量相对论性电子因Klein隧穿可无阻碍穿过静电势垒，难以被囚禁；该困难同样存在于原子塌缩与强子禁闭等问题中。作者希望找到一种不依赖质量势垒、体隙或物理边缘的新囚禁机制。

Method: 以扭转三层石墨烯为平台，利用其Bernal与菱方畴由平滑边界相接的结构，使两类载流子直接接触。结合数值模拟与解析建模，分析无质量载流子在两种堆垛畴之间的绝热连续性，以及经非绝热耦合到有质量载流子的逃逸过程。

Result: 无质量载流子不存在进入菱方畴的绝热延拓；其通过与有质量载流子的非绝热耦合逃逸，在平滑堆垛变化下呈指数抑制。绝热极限下实现完美反射，使Bernal畴成为无隙、无边缘金属中的量子腔；态密度出现显著振荡，编码Eisenstein整数算术，将能级与简并度联系到范数与素因数分解。

Conclusion: 该工作提出一种区别于既有方案的无质量费米子囚禁机制，把相对论性囚禁与数论引入可实验探测的电子能谱，为在无隙石墨烯体系中研究类原子塌缩与禁闭现象提供新途径。

Abstract: Electrons in graphene move like massless relativistic particles, making them exceptionally mobile but difficult to trap because Klein tunnelling permits unimpeded passage through electrostatic barriers. This challenge extends to atomic collapse and hadron confinement. Twisted trilayers form Bernal and rhombohedral domains joined by smooth boundaries, bringing their carriers into contact. Here we show that a new mechanism, distinct from established approaches, confines massless fermions in equilateral triangular Bernal domains without mass barriers, bulk gaps or physical edges. Simulations and analytical modeling reveal that massless carriers have no adiabatic continuation into rhombohedral domains, while escape through nonadiabatic coupling to massive carriers is exponentially suppressed by smooth stacking variation. Perfect reflection in the adiabatic limit turns Bernal domains into quantum cavities in a gapless, edgeless metal. Striking density-of-states oscillations encode Eisenstein-integer arithmetic, linking energies and degeneracies to norms and prime factorizations. These findings bring relativistic confinement and number theory into experimentally accessible electronic spectra.

</details>


### [109] [Density Saturation and Autocorrelation Characteristics of Interlayer Exciton Ensembles in MoSe$_2$/WSe$_2$ Heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28158)
*Mirco Troue,Johannes Figueiredo,Alina Scheuermann,Sebastian Loy,Jonas Kiemle,Takashi Taniguchi,Kenji Watanabe,Ursula Wurstbauer,Alexander W. Holleitner*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 通过时间分辨光致发光与二阶自相关测量，在MoSe₂/WSe₂异质结中提取层间激子密度-温度图，观测到约10¹³ cm⁻²的密度饱和（与莫特转变预言相符），且g⁽²⁾(0)≈1，符合泊松统计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究范德华异质结中层间激子系综是否存在密度上限（莫特转变），并检验该密度极限是否伴随光子统计性质的偏离。

Method: 对MoSe₂/WSe₂异质结进行时间分辨光致发光与二阶自相关测量，覆盖宽激发功率与不同浴温；以最低发光能层间激子态的PL幅度与寿命提取激子密度，构建密度-温度图。

Result: 在约10¹³ cm⁻²处激子密度出现饱和，与范德华异质结层间激子莫特转变的理论预言一致；二阶自相关显示从低密度至可及最高密度均保持g⁽²⁾(0)≈1。

Conclusion: 最低能层间激子系综在实验可及范围内服从泊松统计，未观测到伴随密度极限的非经典光子统计；同时对莫特转变之外的替代解释进行了讨论。

Abstract: We investigate interlayer excitons in MoSe$_2$/WSe$_2$ heterostructures using time-resolved photoluminescence and second-order autocorrelation measurements across a wide range of excitation powers and bath temperatures. By analyzing the photoluminescence amplitude and lifetime of the interlayer exciton state with the lowest emission energy, we extract a corresponding density-temperature diagram. The extracted exciton density exhibits a saturation at approximately $10^{13}$cm$^{-2}$, consistent with values predicted for a Mott transition of interlayer exciton ensembles in van der Waals heterostructures. Moreover, possible interpretations beyond the Mott transition are discussed. We further test whether the density limit is accompanied by a departure from classical photon statistics. The second-order autocorrelation measurements reveal that the photoluminescence maintains an emission characteristic of $g^{(2)}(0) \approx 1$ from low exciton densities up to the maximum reachable density in the investigated exciton ensembles consistent with the lowest-energy exciton ensemble obeying Poissonian statistics.

</details>


### [110] [Steady-state cooperative emission of interlayer excitons in atomically thin light-emitting diodes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28304)
*Andrés M. Mier Valdivia,Pavel E. Dolgirev,Grace H. Chen,Nadine Leisgang,Andrew Y. Joe,Stefan Ostermann,Deepankur Thureja,Yongxin Zeng,Dapeng Ding,Daniel Rhodes,Bumho Kim,Song Liu,Takashi Taniguchi,Kenji Watanabe,James C. Hone,Susanne F. Yelin,Hongkun Park,Mikhail D. Lukin,Philip Kim*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作实现原子级薄TMD发光二极管，通过边缘增强的 fringe 电场局域电注入层间激子，观测到稳态超辐射光子的集体衰迹象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当发射体集合被限制在一个光波长内时，辐射会关联并出现合作发光。二维TMD电子-空穴双层中，强光-物质耦合可形成长寿命偶极的层间激子（IXs），为涌现集体现象提供理想平台；作者希望以电驱动方式实现并探测稳态合作发光。

Method: 构建原子级薄LED，利用边缘增强的 fringe 电场把电注入的IXs限制在样品边缘；用Hanbury-Brown–Twiss测量光子-光子关联，考察自由载流子掺杂形成简并费米海以及引入局域电子对关联特征的影响，并与多体量子主方程建模非相干注入IXs稳态超辐射光子统计进行对照。

Result: 观察到集体衰变的显著特征：大幅光子聚束（bunching），随后有限延迟的反聚束（antibunching）与聚束复现（revivals）；当掺杂自由载流子形成简并费米海时这些关联特征消失，而引入局域电子时则增强，结果与多体量子主方程预测一致。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了二维TMD LED中的稳态合作发光，为研究电驱动、非平衡玻色-费米混合相中的强光-物质相互作用提供平台。

Abstract: When an ensemble of emitters is confined within an optical wavelength, its radiation can become correlated, leading to cooperative emission. In two-dimensional (2D) transition metal dichalcogenide (TMD) electron-hole bilayers, strong light-matter coupling enables the formation of interlayer excitons (IXs), long-lived dipolar excitations that provide an ideal setting for emergent collective phenomena. Here, we engineer an atomically thin light-emitting diode (LED) that generates electroluminescence (EL) from electrically injected IXs that are confined near the sample edges by an edge-enhanced fringing electric field. By measuring Hanbury-Brown-Twiss photon-photon correlations, we find pronounced signatures of collective decay in the form of large photon bunching followed by finite delay antibunching and bunching revivals. These emitter correlation signatures disappear when free charge carriers are doped to form a degenerate Fermi sea but are enhanced by the introduction of localized electrons. The results are consistent with a many-body quantum master equation that models the steady-state superradiant photon statistics of incoherently injected IXs. Our work establishes steady-state cooperative emission in a 2D TMD LED as a platform for studying strong light-matter interactions in electrically driven, non-equilibrium phases of Bose-Fermi mixtures.

</details>


### [111] [Electronic driving of torsional modes in carbon nanotubes via orbital-phonon coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28326)
*Koji Yamada,Wataru Izumida,Mamoru Matsuo,Takeo Kato*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文理论研究了悬浮碳纳米管量子点中扭转模式的电子驱动机制，揭示谷轨道角动量与扭转角速度之间的轨道—旋转耦合可实现声子泵浦与电学读出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索无需电极自旋或谷极化的条件下，利用碳纳米管谷态特性驱动机械扭转模式的可能性。

Method: 构建扭转角速度与谷轨道角动量之间的轨道—旋转耦合；用顺序隧穿的主方程计算稳态电流与扭转声子分布。

Result: 当谷态劈裂与扭转声子能量匹配时，谷轨道跃迁可将模式驱出平衡；接触同时控制声子泵浦与电学读出，可实现正、零或负的电流响应。

Conclusion: 该机制不依赖电极的内禀自旋或谷极化，而依托接触处态依赖隧穿，为纳米管量子点中扭转声子的电控与探测提供方案。

Abstract: We theoretically investigate electronic driving of a torsional mode in a suspended carbon nanotube quantum dot. The valley states in a nanotube carry opposite orbital magnetic moments along the tube axis, and intervalley mixing forms valley-mixed eigenstates whose splitting can be controlled by an axial magnetic field. We formulate an orbital-rotation coupling between the torsional angular velocity and the valley orbital angular momentum. Using a master equation for sequential tunneling through normal-metal electrodes, we calculate the steady-state current and the phonon distribution of the torsional mode. When the valley-state splitting matches the torsional phonon energy, orbital transitions can drive the mode out of equilibrium. The contacts control both phonon pumping and its electrical readout, allowing substantial excitation with a positive, vanishing, or negative current response. The mechanism requires no intrinsic spin or valley polarization of the electrodes, but uses state-dependent tunneling at the contacts.

</details>


### [112] [Spin-wave circulation in unbounded ferromagnetic film](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28334)
*Jan Klima,Daniel Stoeffler,Andres Sarmiento,Kevin Weber,Loic Temdie,Aurélie Solignac,Roberto Fenollosa,Yves Henry,Matthieu Bailleul,Vincent Vlaminck*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 利用硬磁球形微粒的杂散场在连续YIG薄膜中构建径向对称磁化景观，实验与微磁模拟共同证实自旋波可绕微粒循环传播多个回路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋波计算的关键难题是在电路中引导自旋波而不产生波形畸变或反射，现有方案多导致一维传播，限制了在二维磁子电路中的应用。

Method: 实验上在平整YIG薄膜上放置硬磁球形微粒，利用其杂散场形成径向对称的非均匀磁化景观引导自旋波进入闭合路径；并开展再现实验条件的微磁模拟。

Result: 实验观测到自旋波被引导至绕微粒的闭合圆形路径；微磁模拟与实验一致，确认自旋波绕微粒循环数圈。

Conclusion: 证明可利用非均匀磁化景观将自旋波导向圆形路径，为可扩展的自旋波信息处理技术提供新途径。

Abstract: A key challenge in spin-wave computing is steering spin waves through circuits without wave distortion or reflections. This often results in 1D propagation scenarios, which hinder applicability in 2D magnonic circuits. We demonstrate experimentally a novel approach using a hard magnetic spherical microparticle to guide spin-waves onto a closed path in a plain thin YIG film. The particle stray field induces a radially symmetrical magnetization landscape, which allows spin-waves to loop around. In parallel, we conducted micromagnetic simulations reproducing the experimental conditions, which confirm the circulation of spin-waves over several loops around the particle. This work demonstrates the possibility to steer spin-waves onto a circular path using inhomogeneous magnetic landscape, as well as advancing scalable spin-wave-based information processing technologies.

</details>


### [113] [Orbital-angular-momentum control of localized Floquet states and photocurrents in Weyl semimetals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28396)
*Kyle S. Netherwood,Juan I. Aranzadi,Nils Jacobsen,Michael A. Sentef,Sang Soon Oh*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文建立实空间理论，研究涡旋光束驱动的Weyl半金属，证明光学轨道角动量（OAM）可产生高度局域化的Floquet涡旋态与显著的环形光电流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构光不仅通过偏振，还可通过空间分布与OAM调控量子材料，但缺乏描述空间结构光驱动Weyl体系光电流的实空间理论框架。

Method: 发展涡旋光束驱动Weyl半金属的实空间理论：利用柱对称性导出类Bogoliubov-de Gennes径向方程，按角动量分辨涡旋态并追踪其在光缀饰Weyl锥间的演化；对Laguerre-Gaussian光束分析局域涡旋态与体态的杂化；辅以保留完整二维空间结构的Wilson格点模型，直接计算局域光电流。

Result: 两种方法一致给出OAM诱导的、局域于光束轴附近的涡旋态；格点计算显示其环流电流密度显著高于扩展的表面态，且最强电流密度对应最局域的低角动量涡旋模式。

Conclusion: 光学OAM可作为产生与操控Weyl体系中局域载流态的可调控制参量，所建立的实空间框架适用于研究空间结构光产生的光电流。

Abstract: Structured light provides a route to controlling quantum materials not only through its polarization, but also through its spatial profile and orbital angular momentum (OAM). We develop real space theories of a Weyl semimetal driven by a vortex beam and show that optical OAM generates highly localized Floquet vortex states with pronounced circulating photocurrents. Exploiting cylindrical symmetry, we derive a Bogoliubov-de Gennes-like radial equation that resolves the states by angular momentum and reveals their evolution between the light-dressed Weyl cones. For Laguerre-Gaussian beams, the spatially varying drive produces strong hybridization between localized vortex states and bulk states. We complement this continuum description with a Wilson-lattice formulation that retains the full two-dimensional spatial structure and enables direct calculation of local photocurrents. Both approaches consistently identify OAM-induced vortex states localized around the beam axis. The lattice calculation further shows that these states support substantially stronger circulating current densities than the more extended surface states, with the largest current densities associated with the most strongly localized, low-angular-momentum vortex modes. Our results establish optical OAM as a tunable control parameter for creating and manipulating localized current-carrying states in Weyl systems and provide a real-space framework for studying photocurrents generated by spatially structured light.

</details>


### [114] [Quantum Formulation of the Spinterface Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28406)
*Seif Alwan*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文在NEGF框架下建立CISS效应的全量子spinterface模型，将自旋流分离为偶（非手性）与奇（手性）两部分，并给出四格点阈值判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CISS实验信号可能被非手性自旋阀机制混淆，缺乏区分"真手性"与"非手性"贡献的判据，因此需要建立可定量分离二者的量子模型。

Method: 构建金原子（局域磁矩）+ 手性分子链（Rashba自旋轨道耦合）+ 双非磁电极的结，采用非平衡格林函数方法，磁矩由非平衡电子分布自洽确定，由平均场哈密顿量计算自旋分辨透射。

Result: 界面磁矩与手性Rashba项协同产生大电流极化，复现CISS主要实验特征：高极化率、四格点阈值出现、随链长干涉调制增长、热抑制、手性反转致符号反转；奇部自旋流在少于四格点时恒为零，四格点（一金原子+三分子格点）为最小非零几何。

Conclusion: 奇部源于同时满足手性、磁不对称（磁矩仅在金端）及至少两条Rashba活性键的互易性破缺，可作为无参数判据检验自旋信号是否真正手性。

Abstract: We present a fully quantum formulation of the spinterface model for the chirality-induced spin selectivity (CISS) effect, implemented within the non-equilibrium Green's function (NEGF) formalism. The model consists of a gold atom hosting a localised magnetic moment, coupled to a chiral molecular chain with Rashba spin-orbit coupling, and contacted by two non-magnetic leads. The magnetic moment is determined self-consistently from the non-equilibrium electronic distribution, and the spin-resolved transmission is computed from the mean-field Hamiltonian. We show that the interfacial magnetic moment and the chiral Rashba term cooperate to produce large current polarisations that reproduce the key experimental signatures of CISS: high polarisation, a threshold emergence at four total sites followed by interference-modulated growth with chain length, thermal suppression, and sign reversal upon chirality inversion. The central new result of this work is a clean separation of the CISS effect into an even (achiral) and an odd (chiral) contribution to the spin current, $I_{S_z}(φ) = I_{S_z}^{\rm even}(φ) + I_{S_z}^{\rm odd}(φ)$. We demonstrate that $I_{S_z}^{\rm odd}$ vanishes exactly at fixed $m_z$ for chains of fewer than four sites and becomes non-zero for chains of four or more sites, with the smallest junction exhibiting a non-zero odd part being the four-site geometry (one gold atom plus three molecular sites). The odd part arises from reciprocity breaking in a device that is simultaneously chiral, magnetically asymmetric (moment on the gold end only), and long enough to support two or more Rashba-active bonds. This provides a direct, parameter-free test of whether an observed spin signal is genuinely chiral or arises from an achiral spin-valve mechanism.

</details>


### [115] [Grain-Boundary Scattering between Chiral Domains in Chiral Topological Semimetal CoSi](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28426)
*Nicholas A. Lanzillo,Ion Garate,Ching-Tzu Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文用第一性原理量子输运计算比较CoSi薄膜中孪晶（异手性）与非孪晶（同手性）晶界的电子散射性质，发现孪晶晶界电阻更高、表面导电占比更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手性拓扑半金属在下一代纳米互连中有潜在应用价值，但其薄膜中晶界（尤其是手性相关的孪晶/非孪晶晶界）对电子输运的影响尚不清楚，需要阐明其散射机制。

Method: 采用第一性原理量子输运计算，构建含孪晶晶界（异手性）与非孪晶晶界（同手性）的CoSi薄膜模型，比较跨晶界散射的电阻、表面导电占比，并结合透射本征态分布分析散射路径。

Result: 跨孪晶晶界的散射电阻高于非孪晶晶界；非孪晶晶界的表面导电占比更高。透射本征态显示，表面费米弧电子可几乎无显著背散射地穿过同一表面上同手性畴之间的非孪晶晶界，而在异手性畴之间的孪晶晶界处被散射至对面表面，形成部分前向透射与部分背散射，导致更高晶界电阻。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了手性拓扑半金属中晶界手性构型对电子输运的关键调控作用，为理解其输运复杂性及纳米互连应用提供了微观机制依据。

Abstract: We evaluate the electron scattering properties in CoSi thin films having either twin (heterochiral) or non-twin (homochiral) grain boundaries using first-principles quantum transport calculations. We find that scattering across a twin grain boundary is more resistive than scattering across a non-twin grain boundary and that non-twin grain boundaries have a greater percentage of surface conduction than twin grain boundaries. This is corroborated by the transmission eigenstate profiles, which show that the surface Fermi-arc electrons can transmit past the non-twin grain boundaries between homochiral domains on the same surface without significant backscattering. In contrast, these surface electrons get scattered along the twin grain boundaries between heterochiral domains to the opposite surface, resulting in partial forward transmission and partial backscattering that yields higher grain-boundary resistivity. This work helps elucidate the complexities of electron transport in chiral topological semimetals for potential application in next-generation nano-interconnects.

</details>


### [116] [Elastic field causing noncommutativity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.08604)
*A. L. Silva Netto,A. M. de M. Carvalho,G. Q. Garcia,C. Furtado*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 研究均匀挠率背景（模拟连续螺型位错密度）如何诱导有效空间非对易性并重塑自由量子粒子的能谱，在强耦合下得到类朗道能级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将几何缺陷理论中的挠率与空间非对易性联系起来，厘清挠率强度如何设定非对易尺度并主导向平直空间朗道极限的过渡。

Method: 在几何缺陷理论框架下，用度规导出横向动力学中的一阶（类磁）耦合，等效磁场 B_eff ∝ p_z·Ω；在强耦合（朗道）区域分析平面坐标满足 [x,y]≠0 的能谱结构。

Result: 能谱组织为类朗道能级，并出现电场驱动的轻微倾斜与整体均匀平移；随 Ω 增大，系统连续趋于平直空间的朗道问题。

Conclusion: 挠率充当非对易尺度的来源，并控制着向朗道极限的逼近，为几何缺陷与空间非对易性之间提供了统一的谱学描述。

Abstract: We study how a uniform torsion background, modeling a continuous density of screw dislocations and induces effective spatial noncommutativity and reshapes the energy spectrum of a free quantum particle. Within the geometric theory of defects, the metric yields a first-order (magnetic-like) coupling in the transverse dynamics, equivalent to an effective magnetic field $B_{eff}$ proportional to $p_z Omega$, where $Omega$ encodes the torsion strength. In the strong-coupling (Landau) regime, the planar coordinates obey [x,y] != 0 and the spectrum organizes into Landau-like levels with a slight electric-field-driven tilt and a uniform shift. Thus, increasing $Omega$ drives the system continuously toward the familiar Landau problem in flat space, with torsion setting the noncommutativity scale and controlling the approach to the Landau limit.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.dis-nn'></div>

# cond-mat.dis-nn [[Back]](#toc)

### [117] [Exact spectral integrability of a finite-channel random Schrödinger operator: from fermionization of the Dyson--Riccati gas to Kontsevich integral](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27154)
*Yan V. Fyodorov,Pierre Le Doussal*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该文精确求解了任意有限通道数N、β=2酉对称类下准一维随机矩阵薛定谔算子（厄米特白噪声势）的Riccati动力学，并通过单个Airy Hankel τ函数统一给出态密度与Lyapunov增长率，揭示大N谱边的Painlevé I三重拐点普适软化标度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作仅对单通道(N=1)情形有Halperin–Thouless精确公式，缺乏对任意通道数下准一维多通道随机介质中Riccati本征值气体、态密度及Lyapunov指数的精确非微扰刻画，尤其缺乏对大N谱边软化机制的统一定量描述。

Method: 将Dyson–Riccati本征值气体在任意样本长度L上精确表示为自由费米子；取L→∞极限，用单个Airy Hankel τ函数表达态密度与总Lyapunov增长率，并给出其外向围道Kontsevich矩阵Airy积分表示；进一步作鞍点分析与双标度分析。

Result: 获得多通道N=1公式的推广；统一导出有限通道密度Lifshitz尾与大N谱边行为：N≫1时，N=∞的锐边在N^{-4/5}谱窗内被软化，其剖面由Painlevé I的tritronquée解支配。

Conclusion: 作者猜想该准一维标度谱边剖面对于一大类β=2随机带矩阵具有普适性。

Abstract: We solve exactly, for arbitrary finite channel number $N$, the Riccati dynamics of a quasi-one-dimensional random matrix Schrödinger operator with Hermitian white-noise potential in the $β=2$ unitary symmetry class. The associated Dyson--Riccati eigenvalue gas admits an exact free-fermion representation at any sample length $L$, which in the limit $L\to \infty$ expresses the density of states and the total Lyapunov growth rate exactly through a single Airy Hankel $τ$-function providing multichannel generalization of $N=1$ Halperin--Thouless formulas. The $τ-$function further admits an outgoing-contour Kontsevich matrix Airy integral representation. Its saddle-point and double-scaling analyses give, in a unified way, the finite-channel density Lifshitz tail, and the large-$N$ spectral edge. In particular, for $N\gg 1$ the sharp $N=\infty$ edge is softened within an $N^{-4/5}$ spectral window, with its profile governed by the tritronquée solution of Painlevé I. We conjecture universality of this quasi-one-dimensional scaling edge profile for a broad class of $β=2$ random band matrices.

</details>


### [118] [Moments of Random Permanents and Hafnians: A Replica Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.27872)
*Paolo Facchi,Angelo Mariano,Giorgio Parisi,Saverio Pascazio,Antonello Scardicchio*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 利用permanent与Hafnian的围道积分表示，将随机矩阵矩的计算转化为类似平均场自旋玻璃配分函数矩的复本计算，得到各阶矩的闭式结果及分布的大偏差结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机矩阵的permanent与Hafnian的矩（尤其高阶矩与$1/N$修正）缺乏系统解析方法，而它们直接对应玻色采样（单光子与压缩光）的统计行为。

Method: 用permanent与Hafnian的围道积分表示，把$N\times N$独立元素随机矩阵第$2k$阶矩的求期望转化为复本（replica）计算，并对鞍点做复本对称分析。

Result: 对$k>2$主导鞍点为复本对称，得到指数增长率、前因子及首个$1/N$修正的闭式，与精确枚举一致；增长率仅取决于元素为实或复，高阶累积量只影响前因子与$1/N$项。$k=2$时复本对称解失稳，将幂律体与大幅偏差尾部区分开，并给出后者。

Conclusion: permanent与Hafnian的矩可通过复本对称鞍点法精确刻画，结果可应用于单光子与压缩光的玻色采样。

Abstract: Using contour-integral representations of the permanent and of the Hafnian, we turn the computation of their moments for random matrices into a replica calculation, analogous to that of the moments of the partition function of a mean-field spin glass. For the $2k$-th moment of $N\times N$ matrices with independent entries, the dominant saddle point is replica symmetric for $k>2$, and we obtain in closed form the exponential growth rate, the prefactor and the first $1/N$ correction, in agreement with exact enumerations. The growth rate depends only on whether the entries are real or complex; higher cumulants affect only the prefactor and the $1/N$ term. At $k=2$ the replica-symmetric solution becomes unstable, separating a power-law bulk of the distribution from a large-deviation tail, which we compute. The results apply to boson sampling with single photons and with squeezed light.

</details>


### [119] [Ubiquitous Flat Bands in Edge-Inflated Random Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28461)
*Richard Berkovits*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该文证明平带并不需要晶格周期性：通过对非周期随机图做"边膨胀"（把每条边替换为一维紧束缚链或相同的无环motif），可普遍产生广泛的平带，并给出其能量、简并度与鲁棒性规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平带传统上与周期性格点几何、破坏性干涉或手征对称约束相联系，通常被认为需要晶体序。作者希望检验平带能否在完全无周期的随机网络中出现，并寻找一种不依赖特定晶格结构的通用生成机制。

Method: 提出边膨胀（edge-inflation）与motif替换程序：以随机正则图、Voronoi镶嵌、Delaunay三角剖分、无标度网络、小世界网络和Erdős–Rényi图为种子图，将每条边替换为长度固定为L或平均长度为L̄的随机长度一维紧束缚链（或一般性的相同无环motif）。平带能量由链上或motif的本征谱决定；本征态为膨胀链子集上由种子顶点处破坏性干涉形成的局域态。对零能平带，将简并度与图零度η(G)、最大匹配数ν(G)关联，并用数值检验η(G)=N−2ν(G)。最后考察种子格点无序、随机磁通、键无序与链/motif格点无序对平带的影响。

Result: 尽管原始随机图邻接矩阵通常满秩、零本征态极少或没有，其膨胀版本却出现广延平带。零能态简并度主要由种子图的平均度控制；数值结果在各随机图集合上支持η(G)=N−2ν(G)（仅有小偏差）。鲁棒性呈选择性：种子格点无序与随机磁通基本不改变平带，而键无序及链/motif格点无序会使平带展宽。

Conclusion: 平带不依赖晶体序，而是局部图膨胀与motif替换的稳健结果；这为在无序、非周期网络中设计平带提供了一般性原理。

Abstract: Flat bands are usually associated with periodic lattice geometries, where destructive interference or chiral-symmetry constraints produce macroscopically degenerate eigenstates. Here we show that flat bands arise generically in a broad class of nonperiodic random networks through a simple edge-inflation procedure. Starting from random regular, Voronoi tessellation, Delaunay triangulation, scale-free, small-world, and Erdös-Rényi seed graphs, each edge is replaced by a finite one-dimensional tight-binding chain, either with fixed length $L$ or with random lengths of mean $\bar L$. Although the original random graphs typically have adjacency matrices of full rank and therefore few or no zero eigenstates, their inflated counterparts exhibit extensive flat bands. The flat-band energies are determined by the eigenstates residing on the chains or, more generally, by the eigenvalues of identical loopless motifs replacing the seed bonds. The corresponding eigenstates are states supported on subsets of the inflated chains formed by destructive interference at the original seed vertices. For zero-energy flat bands, we relate the degeneracy to the graph nullity $η(G)$ and the maximum matching number $ν(G)$. The numerical results support the correspondence $η(G)=N-2ν(G)$, up to small deviations, across the random graph ensembles studied. The fraction of degenerate zero-energy states in inflated random graphs is controlled primarily by the average degree of the seed graph. We further show that the flat bands exhibit characteristic robustness to disorder: disorder on the seed sites and random magnetic flux leave the flat bands essentially unchanged, while bond disorder and disorder on the chain or motif sites broaden them. These results show that flat bands do not require crystalline order, but can emerge as robust consequences of local graph inflation and motif substitution.

</details>
